# P5 Tree Core Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Real family trees. Editors add people and marriages, the Owner (shown as "Manager") picks the progenitor, everyone sees the tree drawn as a scrapbook board, changes survive a reload, and two people saving at once are detected (409). The Owner can also delete a whole family tree.

**Architecture:**
- **One pure function for every change.** `packages/shared/src/tree/` (imported as `@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`) holds the Zod schemas of Person, Union and DateInfo, the operation union and `applyOp(tree, op) → { tree, inverse }`. The browser runs it for optimistic updates, and the API runs the same function inside a MongoDB transaction, so the two can never disagree about a business rule. Every operation returns its inverse, which the API stores in the operation log (undo and history arrive in P6).
- **Tree shape.** `TreeState = { progenitorId, persons: Record<id, Person>, unions: Record<id, Union> }`. Records keyed by id give O(1) lookups and are plain JSON (the API response, the Zustand store and test fixtures use the same shape). `applyOp` is immutable with structural sharing: it copies the one record it changes and keeps every untouched Person and Union object, so the API finds what to write by comparing references, and a React card re-renders only when its own Person object changes.
- **Derived values.** `buildTreeIndex`, `computeGenerations` and `classifyLineage` walk the tree once from the progenitor (O(n) after one sort): blood relatives descend through `parentUnionId`, in-laws are partners of a blood member, everyone else is "not yet linked".
- **API.** Three Mongo collections (Person, Union, Operation with a unique `(clanId, seq)`) and two new Clan fields (`version`, `progenitorId`). `POST /api/clans/:id/ops` checks the version, loads the tree, runs `applyOp`, writes only the changed documents, appends the Operation and bumps the version, all in one transaction. `GET /api/clans/:id/tree` reads everything in one transaction, so the version always matches the data. `DELETE /api/clans/:id` removes the clan and every clan-scoped document.
- **Web.** `features/tree/` adds a Zustand store (`useTreeStore`) with an optimistic tree, the confirmed tree and a queue of pending ops sent one at a time; a pure layout (§7.4 steps 1–4) on the main thread; and a React Flow board that reuses the P2 scrapbook primitives (Polaroid, Thread, the stitch style). Editing is minimal: add the first person, add a child, add a spouse, delete, and (Owner) set the progenitor. On 409 the store reloads the whole tree; the rebase of §6.3 is P6.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 6, Zod 4, fast-check 4 (tests), Express 5, Mongoose 9.12 (transactions on `MongoMemoryReplSet` in tests), React 19, React Router 8, TanStack Query 5, Zustand 5.1, @xyflow/react 12.12, Vitest 5, Testing Library, Playwright 1.65.

**Spec:** [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-11-ancestors-tree-design.md`](../specs/2026-11-11-ancestors-tree-design.md) §3 (permissions), §5 (Person, Union, Operation, Clan.version/progenitorId, derived values), §6.1–6.3, §7.1–7.4, §8 (classification and display rules), §13.1–13.3, §15. Roadmap: [`2026-11-12-roadmap.md`](2026-11-12-roadmap.md), P5 row and "P5 — Tree core" carry-over.

**Decisions made with the user for P5**
- One PR; tasks run one at a time.
- The add-person form asks only full name, gender and living/deceased. Dates arrive with `DualDateInput` in P6.
- The Owner chooses the progenitor with a new `setProgenitor { personId | null }` op (also inside a `batch`, Owner only).
- On 409 the client reloads the whole tree and says so; the rebase of §6.3 lands in P6.
- `setTreeLink` (P10) and `setTreeTheme` (P6b) are not in the op union yet; `treeLink`, `photo` and `cardStyle` are not in the P5 Person schema.
- Signed-in visitors of `/` go to their family trees (`/clans`); the sample board stays for signed-out visitors.

**Decisions made while planning** (the controller reports them to the user)
- **Rule violations carry a reason.** `RULE_VIOLATION` (422) bodies gain an optional `reason` (`PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN`, `CYCLE`, …), listed in `docs/error-codes.md` by the generator and translated in the web locales.
- **Malformed ids are a validation error.** The request schema rejects a malformed ObjectId with `VALIDATION_ERROR` (400) before `applyOp` runs; an id that is already used anywhere is `RULE_VIOLATION` / `DUPLICATE_ID`. The spec said `RULE_VIOLATION` for both; Task 15 fixes the text.
- **A Union that still has children cannot be deleted** (`UNION_HAS_CHILDREN`), so no child is ever left pointing at a missing Union. The spec did not say.
- **Limits.** A `batch` holds 1–500 ops and cannot contain another `batch`; a tree holds at most 5,000 people (`TREE_FULL`, the §5 design limit); `GET /ops?since=` returns at most 500 entries per call. The ops endpoint uses the existing per-user budget (300 requests/minute) and the global 1 MB JSON body limit; no new limiter.
- **Layout limits (P7 refines).** A marriage between two blood relatives is drawn under the partner reached first (a knot under the card, no stitch); an in-law married to two blood relatives is drawn once, beside the first. People not linked to the progenitor are listed in the "Not yet linked" tray, not drawn on the board.
- **The store keeps `tree` as one object** (`tree.persons`, `tree.unions`, `tree.progenitorId`) instead of three top-level fields, because `applyOp` returns a whole `TreeState`.
- **After creating a family tree the app opens its tree page** (the empty state says "Add the first person"); `e2e/accounts.spec.ts` now clicks the Members tab.

## Global Constraints

**Repository and language**
- Node `>=24.0.0`, `"packageManager": "pnpm@11.17.0"`, TypeScript `~6.0.3` (do not upgrade). Use plain `pnpm` (not `rtk pnpm`). Run every command from the repository root.
- Everything in the repository is in English (code, identifiers, comments, docs, commit messages). The UI is Vietnamese by default with English; user-facing text lives only in `apps/web/src/locales/{vi,en}.json` with identical key sets (`locales.test.ts` enforces it).
- The spec is bilingual: any change to `2026-11-11-ancestors-tree-design.md` updates `2026-11-11-ancestors-tree-design.vi.md` in the same commit.
- User-facing names (spec §3): a Clan is a **family tree** ("cây gia phả"), the `owner` role is **Manager** ("Quản lý"). Code, API paths and the database keep `clan` and `owner`. The progenitor is "thuỷ tổ"; unlinked people are listed under "Chưa nối vào cây" / "Not yet linked".
- API source runs natively on Node (ADR 0002): relative imports end in `.ts`; `erasableSyntaxOnly` forbids enums, namespaces with values and constructor parameter properties (also in web and shared code). Web imports are relative without extensions.
- Web features import shared UI only through `apps/web/src/shared/ui/index.ts`, and other features only through their `index.ts`.
- All tree logic lives in `packages/shared/src/tree/` and is imported as `@ancestors-tree/shared/tree` (a new `exports` entry). Do not re-export it from `packages/shared/src/index.ts`. API contracts (`TreeResponseSchema`, `SubmitOpRequestSchema`, …) live in `packages/shared/src/api/tree.ts` and are exported from the root, like the other API contracts.
- `packages/shared/src/tree/**` is pure: no I/O, no `Date.now()` except the default argument of `newObjectId`, no global mutable state except `newObjectId`'s counter. Algorithms stay O(n) per call for the 5,000-person design limit.

**Secrets and privacy (never break these)**
- Never read, print or edit `apps/api/.env`.
- No credential-shaped strings in tests or docs: no `user:password@host` connection strings, no realistic API keys or tokens.

**API rules (from P3)**
- Every route declares `access`; handlers return `{ status, body }` and never touch `res`; response bodies are parsed through their declared schemas.
- Error responses use only codes from `ERROR_CODES`. Run `pnpm docs:errors` after changing `packages/shared/src/errors.ts` or `errors-doc.ts`; `pnpm check` runs `docs:errors:check`.
- Every Mongoose model is listed in `MODELS` (`apps/api/src/db/indexes.ts`); `indexes.test.ts` fails otherwise.
- Inside a transaction, run database calls one after another (no `Promise.all` on one session).

**Web rules (from P2/P3)**
- Colors only from tokens; icons only from `lucide-react` with `strokeWidth={1.75}` and `aria-hidden="true"`; no emoji.
- Every control is keyboard-reachable with a visible focus outline and an accessible name.
- Component tests use `const user = userEvent.setup()`, `mockApi` from `apps/web/src/test/api.ts` and `vi.unstubAllGlobals()` in `afterEach`.
- Read the Zustand store only through selectors that return a stable value (a primitive or an object already in the store). Never return a new object or array from a selector.

**Tests and commits**
- TDD in every task: write the failing test, run it and see it fail, write the code, run it and see it pass.
- `pnpm check` passes at the end of every task (format, lint, typecheck, tests with coverage, error docs, build). Coverage thresholds: 70% lines overall, 90% for `packages/shared/src/**` (root `vitest.config.ts`).
- Before each commit, format new and changed files with `pnpm exec prettier --write <files>`; the code in this plan is not guaranteed to match Prettier's line breaks.
- One commit per task, Conventional Commits. Write the message to `.superpowers/sdd/p5-task-N-commit.txt` (git-ignored by the `sdd/` rule) and commit with `git commit -F <file>`. Every message ends with the trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

### Task 1: Shared tree schemas, ids and `TreeState`

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/shared/package.json` (exports)
- Create: `packages/shared/src/tree/ids.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/schemas.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/state.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/index.ts`
- Test: `packages/shared/src/tree/ids.test.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/schemas.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `IdSchema` (`packages/shared/src/api/common.ts`); `SolarDateSchema`, `LunarDateSchema`, `MIN_YEAR`, `MAX_YEAR` (`packages/shared/src/lunar/dates.ts`).
- Produces (`@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`):
  - `newObjectId(now?: number): string` — 24 lowercase hex characters (4-byte seconds, 5 random bytes per process, 3-byte counter); `isObjectId(value: unknown): value is string`.
  - `MAX_PERSONS = 5000`.
  - Schemas and types: `GenderSchema`/`Gender` (`'male' | 'female'`), `FullNameSchema`, `DateInfoSchema`/`DateInfo`, `DeathSchema`/`Death`, `BirthFamilySchema`/`BirthFamily`, `PersonSchema`/`Person`, `PersonChangesSchema`/`PersonChanges`, `CLEARABLE_PERSON_FIELDS`, `UnionStatusSchema`/`UnionStatus`, `UnionSchema`/`Union`, `UnionChangesSchema`/`UnionChanges`.
  - `Person = { id, fullName, aliases: string[], gender, birth?, death: { isDeceased, date? }, parentUnionId?, isAdopted, siblingOrder?, branch?, birthFamily?, bio?, burialPlace? }`.
  - `Union = { id, partnerIds: string[] (1–2, distinct), order: number (1–99), status: 'married' | 'divorced' | 'widowed' }`.
  - `interface TreeState { readonly progenitorId: string | null; readonly persons: Readonly<Record<string, Person>>; readonly unions: Readonly<Record<string, Union>> }`, `EMPTY_TREE`, `treeStateFromLists(progenitorId, persons, unions): TreeState`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/ids.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { isObjectId, newObjectId } from './ids.ts';

describe('newObjectId', () => {
  it('makes 24 lowercase hex characters', () => {
    expect(newObjectId()).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{24}$/);
  });

  it('starts with the creation time in seconds, like a MongoDB ObjectId', () => {
    const id = newObjectId(Date.UTC(2026, 10, 13, 8, 0, 0));
    expect(Number.parseInt(id.slice(0, 8), 16)).toBe(Date.UTC(2026, 10, 13, 8, 0, 0) / 1000);
  });

  it('never repeats within a process', () => {
    const ids = new Set(Array.from({ length: 10_000 }, () => newObjectId(0)));
    expect(ids.size).toBe(10_000);
  });

  it('sorts by creation time', () => {
    const older = newObjectId(Date.UTC(2020, 0, 1));
    const newer = newObjectId(Date.UTC(2030, 0, 1));
    expect(older < newer).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('isObjectId', () => {
  it('accepts 24 lowercase hex characters only', () => {
    expect(isObjectId('64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718')).toBe(true);
    expect(isObjectId('64B7F0C2A1B2C3D4E5F60718')).toBe(false);
    expect(isObjectId('64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6071')).toBe(false);
    expect(isObjectId(42)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/schemas.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  DateInfoSchema,
  DeathSchema,
  PersonChangesSchema,
  PersonSchema,
  UnionChangesSchema,
  UnionSchema,
} from './schemas.ts';
import { EMPTY_TREE, treeStateFromLists } from './state.ts';

const ID = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718';
const OTHER = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60719';

const person = {
  id: ID,
  fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư',
  aliases: [],
  gender: 'male',
  death: { isDeceased: false },
  isAdopted: false,
};

describe('DateInfoSchema', () => {
  it('needs the side the user entered', () => {
    expect(
      DateInfoSchema.safeParse({ source: 'solar', solar: { day: 12, month: 5, year: 1931 } })
        .success,
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(
      DateInfoSchema.safeParse({
        source: 'lunar',
        lunar: { day: 15, month: 10, year: 1931, isLeap: false },
      }).success,
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(DateInfoSchema.safeParse({ source: 'approx', approxYear: 1920 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(DateInfoSchema.safeParse({ source: 'solar', approxYear: 1920 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(DateInfoSchema.safeParse({ source: 'approx', approxYear: 999 }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('reuses the lunar library date checks', () => {
    expect(
      DateInfoSchema.safeParse({ source: 'solar', solar: { day: 30, month: 2, year: 1931 } })
        .success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('DeathSchema', () => {
  it('allows a date of death only for a deceased person', () => {
    const date = { source: 'approx', approxYear: 1990 };
    expect(DeathSchema.safeParse({ isDeceased: true, date }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(DeathSchema.safeParse({ isDeceased: false, date }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('PersonSchema', () => {
  it('accepts a minimal person and trims the name', () => {
    expect(PersonSchema.parse({ ...person, fullName: '  Nguyễn Văn Tư ' })).toEqual(person);
  });

  it('rejects a malformed id, an empty name and an unknown gender', () => {
    expect(PersonSchema.safeParse({ ...person, id: 'abc' }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(PersonSchema.safeParse({ ...person, fullName: '  ' }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(PersonSchema.safeParse({ ...person, gender: 'other' }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('PersonChangesSchema', () => {
  it('takes changed fields only, and null clears an optional field', () => {
    expect(PersonChangesSchema.parse({ fullName: 'Tư', bio: null })).toEqual({
      fullName: 'Tư',
      bio: null,
    });
    expect(PersonChangesSchema.safeParse({}).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('refuses fields that other ops own', () => {
    expect(PersonChangesSchema.safeParse({ id: OTHER }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(PersonChangesSchema.safeParse({ parentUnionId: OTHER }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(PersonChangesSchema.safeParse({ fullName: null }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('UnionSchema', () => {
  it('has one or two different partners', () => {
    const union = { id: OTHER, partnerIds: [ID], order: 1, status: 'married' };
    expect(UnionSchema.safeParse(union).success).toBe(true);
    expect(UnionSchema.safeParse({ ...union, partnerIds: [] }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(UnionSchema.safeParse({ ...union, partnerIds: [ID, ID] }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(UnionSchema.safeParse({ ...union, partnerIds: [ID, OTHER, OTHER] }).success).toBe(
      false,
    );
  });

  it('changes need at least one field', () => {
    expect(UnionChangesSchema.safeParse({ status: 'divorced' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(UnionChangesSchema.safeParse({}).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('treeStateFromLists', () => {
  it('keys people and unions by id', () => {
    const union = { id: OTHER, partnerIds: [ID], order: 1, status: 'married' as const };
    const tree = treeStateFromLists(ID, [PersonSchema.parse(person)], [union]);
    expect(tree.progenitorId).toBe(ID);
    expect(tree.persons[ID]?.fullName).toBe('Nguyễn Văn Tư');
    expect(tree.unions[OTHER]).toEqual(union);
    expect(EMPTY_TREE).toEqual({ progenitorId: null, persons: {}, unions: {} });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the ids**

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/ids.ts`:
```ts
const OBJECT_ID = /^[a-f0-9]{24}$/;

/** True for a MongoDB ObjectId written as 24 lowercase hex characters. */
export function isObjectId(value: unknown): value is string {
  return typeof value === 'string' && OBJECT_ID.test(value);
}

function randomBytes(count: number): Uint8Array {
  // Web Crypto: the same call works in browsers and in Node 24.
  return globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(count));
}

function toHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  return Array.from(bytes, (byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

/** Five random bytes per process (one browser tab or one API instance), as in MongoDB. */
const PROCESS_PART = toHex(randomBytes(5));
let counter = new DataView(randomBytes(4).buffer).getUint32(0) & 0xffffff;

/**
 * A new ObjectId made on the client, so an optimistic edit can show the new person at once
 * (spec §5): 4 bytes of seconds since 1970, 5 random bytes, a 3-byte counter.
 */
export function newObjectId(now: number = Date.now()): string {
  counter = (counter + 1) & 0xffffff;
  const seconds = Math.floor(now / 1000) >>> 0;
  return `${seconds.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}${PROCESS_PART}${counter.toString(16).padStart(6, '0')}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the schemas and the tree state**

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/schemas.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { IdSchema } from '../api/common.ts';
import { LunarDateSchema, MAX_YEAR, MIN_YEAR, SolarDateSchema } from '../lunar/dates.ts';

/** The design limit of one family tree (spec §5). */
export const MAX_PERSONS = 5000;

export const GenderSchema = z.enum(['male', 'female']);
// No custom messages: the web translates Zod issues by code (formErrors.ts).
export const FullNameSchema = z.string().trim().min(1).max(120);
const ShortTextSchema = z.string().trim().max(120);
const AliasesSchema = z.array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(120)).max(10);
const SiblingOrderSchema = z.number().int().min(1).max(99);
const BioSchema = z.string().trim().max(5000);
const BurialPlaceSchema = z.string().trim().max(200);

/**
 * A birth or death date (spec §5). The lunar library's schemas are nested, not extended: Zod 4
 * cannot extend a refined object schema.
 */
export const DateInfoSchema = z
  .object({
    solar: SolarDateSchema.optional(),
    lunar: LunarDateSchema.optional(),
    approxYear: z.number().int().min(MIN_YEAR).max(MAX_YEAR).optional(),
    /** What the user actually entered; kept as the ground truth. */
    source: z.enum(['solar', 'lunar', 'approx']),
  })
  .refine(
    (date) =>
      (date.source === 'solar'
        ? date.solar
        : date.source === 'lunar'
          ? date.lunar
          : date.approxYear) !== undefined,
    { message: 'The side the user entered must be present', path: ['source'] },
  );

export const DeathSchema = z
  .object({ isDeceased: z.boolean(), date: DateInfoSchema.optional() })
  .refine((death) => death.isDeceased || death.date === undefined, {
    message: 'Only a deceased person has a date of death',
    path: ['date'],
  });

/** Only meaningful for in-laws (spec §8). */
export const BirthFamilySchema = z.object({
  surname: ShortTextSchema.optional(),
  fatherName: ShortTextSchema.optional(),
  motherName: ShortTextSchema.optional(),
  hometown: ShortTextSchema.optional(),
  note: z.string().trim().max(1000).optional(),
});

/**
 * A person in the genealogy (spec §5). `clanId`, `createdAt` and `updatedAt` stay on the
 * server. `treeLink` (P10), `photo` (P6) and `cardStyle` (P6b) arrive with their features.
 */
export const PersonSchema = z.object({
  id: IdSchema,
  fullName: FullNameSchema,
  aliases: AliasesSchema,
  gender: GenderSchema,
  birth: DateInfoSchema.optional(),
  death: DeathSchema,
  /** The marriage this person was born into; changed only by `setParents`. */
  parentUnionId: IdSchema.optional(),
  isAdopted: z.boolean(),
  siblingOrder: SiblingOrderSchema.optional(),
  branch: ShortTextSchema.optional(),
  birthFamily: BirthFamilySchema.optional(),
  bio: BioSchema.optional(),
  burialPlace: BurialPlaceSchema.optional(),
});

/** Optional Person fields that `updatePerson` may clear by sending `null`. */
export const CLEARABLE_PERSON_FIELDS = [
  'birth',
  'siblingOrder',
  'branch',
  'birthFamily',
  'bio',
  'burialPlace',
] as const;

/** Changed fields only (spec §6.1). `id` and `parentUnionId` belong to other ops. */
export const PersonChangesSchema = z
  .strictObject({
    fullName: FullNameSchema.optional(),
    aliases: AliasesSchema.optional(),
    gender: GenderSchema.optional(),
    birth: DateInfoSchema.nullable().optional(),
    death: DeathSchema.optional(),
    isAdopted: z.boolean().optional(),
    siblingOrder: SiblingOrderSchema.nullable().optional(),
    branch: ShortTextSchema.nullable().optional(),
    birthFamily: BirthFamilySchema.nullable().optional(),
    bio: BioSchema.nullable().optional(),
    burialPlace: BurialPlaceSchema.nullable().optional(),
  })
  .refine((changes) => Object.values(changes).some((value) => value !== undefined), {
    message: 'Send at least one field to change',
  });

export const UnionStatusSchema = z.enum(['married', 'divorced', 'widowed']);

const PartnerIdsSchema = z
  .array(IdSchema)
  .min(1)
  .max(2)
  .refine((ids) => new Set(ids).size === ids.length, {
    message: 'Partners must be different people',
  });
const UnionOrderSchema = z.number().int().min(1).max(99);

/** One marriage (spec §5); one partner when only one parent is known. */
export const UnionSchema = z.object({
  id: IdSchema,
  partnerIds: PartnerIdsSchema,
  /** 1 = first spouse, 2 = second… */
  order: UnionOrderSchema,
  status: UnionStatusSchema,
});

export const UnionChangesSchema = z
  .strictObject({
    partnerIds: PartnerIdsSchema.optional(),
    order: UnionOrderSchema.optional(),
    status: UnionStatusSchema.optional(),
  })
  .refine((changes) => Object.values(changes).some((value) => value !== undefined), {
    message: 'Send at least one field to change',
  });

export type Gender = z.infer<typeof GenderSchema>;
export type DateInfo = z.infer<typeof DateInfoSchema>;
export type Death = z.infer<typeof DeathSchema>;
export type BirthFamily = z.infer<typeof BirthFamilySchema>;
export type Person = z.infer<typeof PersonSchema>;
export type PersonChanges = z.infer<typeof PersonChangesSchema>;
export type UnionStatus = z.infer<typeof UnionStatusSchema>;
export type Union = z.infer<typeof UnionSchema>;
export type UnionChanges = z.infer<typeof UnionChangesSchema>;
```

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/state.ts`:
```ts
import type { Person, Union } from './schemas.ts';

/**
 * A whole family tree. Records keyed by id: O(1) lookups, plain JSON, and immutable updates
 * that copy one record and keep every untouched Person and Union object (structural sharing),
 * so changed documents and changed cards are found by comparing references.
 */
export interface TreeState {
  readonly progenitorId: string | null;
  readonly persons: Readonly<Record<string, Person>>;
  readonly unions: Readonly<Record<string, Union>>;
}

export const EMPTY_TREE: TreeState = { progenitorId: null, persons: {}, unions: {} };

export function treeStateFromLists(
  progenitorId: string | null,
  persons: readonly Person[],
  unions: readonly Union[],
): TreeState {
  return {
    progenitorId,
    persons: Object.fromEntries(persons.map((person) => [person.id, person])),
    unions: Object.fromEntries(unions.map((union) => [union.id, union])),
  };
}
```

```ts
  PersonChangesSchema,

  UnionChangesSchema,
  UnionSchema,
  UnionStatusSchema,
  type BirthFamily,
  type DateInfo,
  type Death,
  type Gender,
  type Person,
  type PersonChanges,
  type Union,
  type UnionChanges,
  type UnionStatus,
} from './schemas.ts';
export { EMPTY_TREE, treeStateFromLists, type TreeState } from './state.ts';
```

### Task 2: Operations, `OpError` and `applyOp`

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/shared/src/tree/op-error.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/ops.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/apply-op.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/testing.ts`
- Modify: `packages/shared/src/tree/index.ts`
- Test: `packages/shared/src/tree/ops.test.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/apply-op.test.ts`, `packages/shared/src/tree/apply-op.properties.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 schemas and types, `TreeState`, `EMPTY_TREE`, `treeStateFromLists`, `isObjectId`, `MAX_PERSONS`; `IdSchema` from `packages/shared/src/api/common.ts`.
- Produces (`@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`):
  - `OP_ERROR_CODES` (code → English description), `OP_ERROR_CODE_LIST: [OpErrorCode, ...OpErrorCode[]]`, `type OpErrorCode = 'INVALID_ID' | 'DUPLICATE_ID' | 'PERSON_NOT_FOUND' | 'UNION_NOT_FOUND' | 'PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN' | 'UNION_HAS_CHILDREN' | 'PROGENITOR_PROTECTED' | 'CYCLE' | 'INVALID_PARTNERS' | 'RESTORE_CONFLICT' | 'TREE_FULL'`.
  - `class OpError extends Error { readonly code: OpErrorCode }`, constructed as `new OpError(code, message?)`.
  - `MAX_BATCH_OPS = 500`; `SingleOpSchema`, `BatchOpSchema`, `OpSchema`; types `SingleOp`, `BatchOp`, `Op`, `OpType`. Every op is `{ type, payload }`:
    `addPerson: Person` · `updatePerson: { id, changes: PersonChanges }` · `deletePerson: { id }` · `addUnion: Union` · `updateUnion: { id, changes: UnionChanges }` · `deleteUnion: { id }` · `setParents: { personId, parentUnionId: string | null }` · `restorePerson: { person: Person, unions: Union[] }` · `restoreUnion: Union` · `setProgenitor: { personId: string | null }` · `batch: { ops: SingleOp[] }` (1–500, no nested batch).
  - `needsOwner(op: Op): boolean` — true for `setProgenitor`, also inside a batch.
  - `applyOp(tree: TreeState, op: Op): ApplyOpResult` where `ApplyOpResult = { tree: TreeState; inverse: Op }` — immutable; throws `OpError`.
  - `isOwnAncestor(tree: TreeState, personId: string): boolean`.
- Produces (internal `packages/shared/src/tree/testing.ts`, imported only by shared tests): `testId(n: number): string`, `makePerson(id, overrides?)`, `makeUnion(id, partnerIds, overrides?)`, `treeOf(persons, unions?, progenitorId?)`, `invariantViolations(tree): string[]`.

Rules `applyOp` enforces (spec §6.1 plus the planning decisions):
- New ids are ObjectIds (`INVALID_ID`) not used by any person or union (`DUPLICATE_ID`). A tree holds at most `MAX_PERSONS` people (`TREE_FULL`).
- Referenced people and unions exist (`PERSON_NOT_FOUND`, `UNION_NOT_FOUND`); a union has one or two different partners (`INVALID_PARTNERS`).
- `deletePerson`: not the progenitor (`PROGENITOR_PROTECTED`), not someone with children in any of their unions (`PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN`). It deletes the unions where the person was the only partner and removes them from shared unions; the inverse `restorePerson` holds the person and every union it deleted or changed, exactly as they were.
- `deleteUnion`: not a union with children (`UNION_HAS_CHILDREN`).
- No cycles (`CYCLE`) after `setParents`, `updateUnion` (new partners) and `restorePerson`. `addPerson`, `addUnion` and `restoreUnion` cannot create one: nothing points at a brand-new person or union yet.
- `restorePerson` fails with `RESTORE_CONFLICT` when a union it puts back has gained a partner the snapshot does not have.
- `batch` applies its ops in order and fails as a whole; its inverse is the batch of inverses in reverse order.

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/testing.ts` (test-only; not exported from `index.ts`):
```ts
import type { Person, Union } from './schemas.ts';
import { treeStateFromLists, type TreeState } from './state.ts';

/** A readable, valid ObjectId for tests: testId(1) = "000…001". */
export function testId(n: number): string {
  return n.toString(16).padStart(24, '0');
}

export function makePerson(id: string, overrides: Partial<Person> = {}): Person {
  return {
    id,
    fullName: `Person ${id.slice(-4)}`,
    aliases: [],
    gender: 'male',
    death: { isDeceased: false },
    isAdopted: false,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

export function makeUnion(id: string, partnerIds: string[], overrides: Partial<Union> = {}): Union {
  return { id, partnerIds, order: 1, status: 'married', ...overrides };
}

export function treeOf(
  persons: Person[],
  unions: Union[] = [],
  progenitorId: string | null = null,
): TreeState {
  return treeStateFromLists(progenitorId, persons, unions);
}

/** Independent of apply-op.ts on purpose: a second, simpler cycle check. */
function isOwnAncestorSlow(tree: TreeState, personId: string): boolean {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const stack = [personId];
  for (let current = stack.pop(); current !== undefined; current = stack.pop()) {
    const unionId = tree.persons[current]?.parentUnionId;
    const union = unionId === undefined ? undefined : tree.unions[unionId];
    for (const parentId of union?.partnerIds ?? []) {
      if (parentId === personId) return true;
      if (!seen.has(parentId)) {
        seen.add(parentId);
        stack.push(parentId);
      }
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** Every broken tree invariant as a short message; [] for a valid tree. */
export function invariantViolations(tree: TreeState): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  for (const [id, person] of Object.entries(tree.persons)) {
    if (person.id !== id) problems.push(`person ${id} is stored under another id`);
    if (person.parentUnionId !== undefined && !tree.unions[person.parentUnionId]) {
      problems.push(`person ${id} points at a missing union`);
    }
    if (isOwnAncestorSlow(tree, id)) problems.push(`person ${id} is their own ancestor`);
  }
  for (const [id, union] of Object.entries(tree.unions)) {
    if (union.id !== id) problems.push(`union ${id} is stored under another id`);
    const partners = union.partnerIds;
    if (partners.length < 1 || partners.length > 2 || new Set(partners).size !== partners.length) {
      problems.push(`union ${id} has wrong partners`);
    }
    for (const partnerId of partners) {
      if (!tree.persons[partnerId]) problems.push(`union ${id} has a missing partner`);
    }
  }
  if (tree.progenitorId !== null && !tree.persons[tree.progenitorId]) {
    problems.push('the progenitor is missing');
  }
  return problems;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MAX_BATCH_OPS, needsOwner, OpSchema, type Op } from './ops.ts';
import { makePerson, makeUnion, testId } from './testing.ts';

const setProgenitor: Op = { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: testId(1) } };
const addPerson: Op = { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(testId(1)) };

describe('OpSchema', () => {
  it('parses every op type', () => {
    const ops: Op[] = [
      addPerson,
      { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: testId(1), changes: { bio: null } } },
      { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: testId(1) } },
      { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(testId(2), [testId(1)]) },
      { type: 'updateUnion', payload: { id: testId(2), changes: { status: 'divorced' } } },
      { type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: testId(2) } },
      { type: 'setParents', payload: { personId: testId(1), parentUnionId: null } },
      { type: 'restorePerson', payload: { person: makePerson(testId(1)), unions: [] } },
      { type: 'restoreUnion', payload: makeUnion(testId(2), [testId(1)]) },
      setProgenitor,
      { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: null } },
      { type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [addPerson, setProgenitor] } },
    ];
    for (const op of ops) expect(OpSchema.parse(op), op.type).toEqual(op);
  });

  it('rejects unknown types, malformed ids, nested, empty and oversized batches', () => {
    expect(OpSchema.safeParse({ type: 'setTreeTheme', payload: {} }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(OpSchema.safeParse({ type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: 'x' } }).success).toBe(false);
    const nested = { type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [addPerson] } };
    expect(OpSchema.safeParse({ type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [nested] } }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(OpSchema.safeParse({ type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [] } }).success).toBe(false);
    const tooMany = Array.from({ length: MAX_BATCH_OPS + 1 }, () => setProgenitor);
    expect(OpSchema.safeParse({ type: 'batch', payload: { ops: tooMany } }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('needsOwner', () => {
  it('is true for setProgenitor, also inside a batch', () => {
    expect(needsOwner(setProgenitor)).toBe(true);
    expect(needsOwner({ type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [addPerson, setProgenitor] } })).toBe(true);
    expect(needsOwner(addPerson)).toBe(false);
    expect(needsOwner({ type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [addPerson] } })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/op-error.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * Why `applyOp` refused a change. The API sends the code as `reason` in a RULE_VIOLATION body,
 * docs/error-codes.md lists these descriptions, and the web translates every code.
 */
export const OP_ERROR_CODES = {
  INVALID_ID: 'An id is not a 24-character lowercase hex ObjectId.',
  DUPLICATE_ID: 'The id of a new person or union is already used.',
  PERSON_NOT_FOUND: 'The person does not exist in this family tree.',
  UNION_NOT_FOUND: 'The union (marriage) does not exist in this family tree.',
  PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN: 'A person who still has children cannot be deleted.',
  UNION_HAS_CHILDREN: 'A union that still has children cannot be deleted.',
  PROGENITOR_PROTECTED: 'The progenitor cannot be deleted; choose another progenitor first.',
  CYCLE: 'The change would make someone their own ancestor.',
  INVALID_PARTNERS: 'A union needs one or two different people (including the restored person).',
  RESTORE_CONFLICT: 'The person cannot be restored: one of their unions has changed partners.',
  TREE_FULL: 'The family tree already has the maximum of 5,000 people.',
} as const satisfies Record<string, string>;

export type OpErrorCode = keyof typeof OP_ERROR_CODES;

export const OP_ERROR_CODE_LIST = Object.keys(OP_ERROR_CODES) as [OpErrorCode, ...OpErrorCode[]];

export class OpError extends Error {
  readonly code: OpErrorCode;

  constructor(code: OpErrorCode, message: string = OP_ERROR_CODES[code]) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'OpError';
    this.code = code;
  }
}
```

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/ops.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { IdSchema } from '../api/common.ts';
import { PersonChangesSchema, PersonSchema, UnionChangesSchema, UnionSchema } from './schemas.ts';

/** Most ops in one batch. */
export const MAX_BATCH_OPS = 500;

const IdPayloadSchema = z.object({ id: IdSchema });

const AddPersonOpSchema = z.object({ type: z.literal('addPerson'), payload: PersonSchema });
const UpdatePersonOpSchema = z.object({
  type: z.literal('updatePerson'),
  payload: z.object({ id: IdSchema, changes: PersonChangesSchema }),
});
const DeletePersonOpSchema = z.object({
  type: z.literal('deletePerson'),
  payload: IdPayloadSchema,
});
const AddUnionOpSchema = z.object({ type: z.literal('addUnion'), payload: UnionSchema });
const UpdateUnionOpSchema = z.object({
  type: z.literal('updateUnion'),
  payload: z.object({ id: IdSchema, changes: UnionChangesSchema }),
});
const DeleteUnionOpSchema = z.object({ type: z.literal('deleteUnion'), payload: IdPayloadSchema });
const SetParentsOpSchema = z.object({
  type: z.literal('setParents'),
  payload: z.object({ personId: IdSchema, parentUnionId: IdSchema.nullable() }),
});
/** Undo of deletePerson: the person plus every union deleted or changed with them. */
const RestorePersonOpSchema = z.object({
  type: z.literal('restorePerson'),
  payload: z.object({ person: PersonSchema, unions: z.array(UnionSchema).max(100) }),
});
const RestoreUnionOpSchema = z.object({ type: z.literal('restoreUnion'), payload: UnionSchema });
/** Owner only (spec §3). */
const SetProgenitorOpSchema = z.object({
  type: z.literal('setProgenitor'),
  payload: z.object({ personId: IdSchema.nullable() }),
});

const SINGLE_OP_SCHEMAS = [
  AddPersonOpSchema,
  UpdatePersonOpSchema,
  DeletePersonOpSchema,
  AddUnionOpSchema,
  UpdateUnionOpSchema,
  DeleteUnionOpSchema,
  SetParentsOpSchema,
  RestorePersonOpSchema,
  RestoreUnionOpSchema,
  SetProgenitorOpSchema,
] as const;

export const SingleOpSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('type', SINGLE_OP_SCHEMAS);

/** Several ops saved as one change (one undo step). A batch cannot contain another batch. */
export const BatchOpSchema = z.object({
  type: z.literal('batch'),
  payload: z.object({ ops: z.array(SingleOpSchema).min(1).max(MAX_BATCH_OPS) }),
});

export const OpSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('type', [...SINGLE_OP_SCHEMAS, BatchOpSchema]);

export type SingleOp = z.infer<typeof SingleOpSchema>;
export type BatchOp = z.infer<typeof BatchOpSchema>;
export type Op = z.infer<typeof OpSchema>;
export type OpType = Op['type'];

/** Choosing the progenitor is the Owner's (spec §3), also inside a batch. */
export function needsOwner(op: Op): boolean {
  if (op.type === 'batch') return op.payload.ops.some((child) => child.type === 'setProgenitor');
  return op.type === 'setProgenitor';
}
```

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/apply-op.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { applyOp, isOwnAncestor } from './apply-op.ts';
import { OpError, type OpErrorCode } from './op-error.ts';
import type { Op } from './ops.ts';
import { MAX_PERSONS, type Person, type Union } from './schemas.ts';
import type { TreeState } from './state.ts';
import { invariantViolations, makePerson, makeUnion, testId, treeOf } from './testing.ts';

const A = testId(1); // progenitor
const B = testId(2); // A's wife, an in-law
const C = testId(3); // their son
const D = testId(4);
const E = testId(5);
const K = testId(6);
const U = testId(101); // A + B, parents of C
const V = testId(102);
const W = testId(103);

/** A + B (union U) with their son C; A is the progenitor. */
function family(): TreeState {
  return treeOf(
    [makePerson(A), makePerson(B, { gender: 'female' }), makePerson(C, { parentUnionId: U })],
    [makeUnion(U, [A, B])],
    A,
  );
}

function expectOpError(run: () => unknown, code: OpErrorCode): void {
  let thrown: unknown;
  try {
    run();
  } catch (error) {
    thrown = error;
  }
  expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(OpError);
  expect((thrown as OpError).code).toBe(code);
}

/** Applies op, checks the input is untouched and that the inverse restores it exactly. */
function roundTrip(tree: TreeState, op: Op): TreeState {
  const before = structuredClone(tree);
  const result = applyOp(tree, op);
  expect(tree).toEqual(before);
  expect(invariantViolations(result.tree)).toEqual([]);
  expect(applyOp(result.tree, result.inverse).tree).toEqual(before);
  return result.tree;
}

describe('addPerson', () => {
  it('adds a person; the inverse deletes them', () => {
    const op: Op = { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(D, { parentUnionId: U }) };
    expect(roundTrip(family(), op).persons[D]?.parentUnionId).toBe(U);
    expect(applyOp(family(), op).inverse).toEqual({ type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: D } });
  });

  it('keeps every untouched object (structural sharing)', () => {
    const tree = family();
    const next = applyOp(tree, { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(D) }).tree;
    expect(next.persons[A]).toBe(tree.persons[A]);
    expect(next.unions).toBe(tree.unions);
  });

  it('refuses malformed and used ids and a missing parent union', () => {
    const add = (person: Person) => () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'addPerson', payload: person });
    expectOpError(add(makePerson('not-an-id')), 'INVALID_ID');
    expectOpError(add(makePerson(A)), 'DUPLICATE_ID');
    expectOpError(add(makePerson(U)), 'DUPLICATE_ID');
    expectOpError(add(makePerson(D, { parentUnionId: V })), 'UNION_NOT_FOUND');
  });

  it('stops at the 5,000-person design limit', () => {
    const full = treeOf(
      Array.from({ length: MAX_PERSONS }, (_, index) => makePerson(testId(index + 1))),
    );
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(full, { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(testId(MAX_PERSONS + 1)) }),
      'TREE_FULL',
    );
  });
});

describe('updatePerson', () => {
  it('changes the given fields, null clears one, the inverse restores the old values', () => {
    const tree = treeOf([makePerson(A, { bio: 'Trưởng họ' })], [], A);
    const op: Op = {
      type: 'updatePerson',
      payload: { id: A, changes: { fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư', bio: null, branch: 'Chi trưởng' } },
    };
    const result = applyOp(tree, op);
    expect(result.tree.persons[A]).toEqual(
      makePerson(A, { fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư', branch: 'Chi trưởng' }),
    );
    expect(result.inverse).toEqual({
      type: 'updatePerson',
      payload: { id: A, changes: { fullName: 'Person 0001', bio: 'Trưởng họ', branch: null } },
    });
    roundTrip(tree, op);
  });

  it('needs an existing person', () => {
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: D, changes: { bio: 'x' } } }),
      'PERSON_NOT_FOUND',
    );
  });
});

describe('deletePerson', () => {
  it('deletes a person without children', () => {
    expect(roundTrip(family(), { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: C } }).persons[C]).toBe(
      undefined,
    );
  });

  it('deletes the unions where they were alone and keeps their spouse in a shared one', () => {
    // D has a one-partner union V and a childless marriage W with E.
    const tree = treeOf(
      [makePerson(A), makePerson(D), makePerson(E, { gender: 'female' })],
      [makeUnion(V, [D]), makeUnion(W, [D, E], { order: 2 })],
      A,
    );
    const op: Op = { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: D } };
    const result = applyOp(tree, op);
    expect(result.tree.unions[V]).toBeUndefined();
    expect(result.tree.unions[W]).toEqual(makeUnion(W, [E], { order: 2 }));
    expect(result.inverse).toEqual({
      type: 'restorePerson',
      payload: {
        person: makePerson(D),
        unions: [makeUnion(V, [D]), makeUnion(W, [D, E], { order: 2 })],
      },
    });
    roundTrip(tree, op);
  });

  it('refuses the progenitor, a parent and a missing person', () => {
    const remove = (id: string) => () =>
      applyOp(family(), { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id } });
    expectOpError(remove(A), 'PROGENITOR_PROTECTED');
    expectOpError(remove(B), 'PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN');
    expectOpError(remove(D), 'PERSON_NOT_FOUND');
  });
});

describe('addUnion', () => {
  it('adds a marriage; the inverse deletes it', () => {
    const tree = applyOp(family(), { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(D) }).tree;
    const op: Op = { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(V, [C, D]) };
    expect(roundTrip(tree, op).unions[V]?.partnerIds).toEqual([C, D]);
    expect(applyOp(tree, op).inverse).toEqual({ type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: V } });
  });

  it('needs one or two different, existing partners and a new id', () => {
    const add = (union: Union) => () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'addUnion', payload: union });
    expectOpError(add(makeUnion(V, [])), 'INVALID_PARTNERS');
    expectOpError(add(makeUnion(V, [A, A])), 'INVALID_PARTNERS');
    expectOpError(add(makeUnion(V, [A, B, C])), 'INVALID_PARTNERS');
    expectOpError(add(makeUnion(V, [A, D])), 'PERSON_NOT_FOUND');
    expectOpError(add(makeUnion(U, [C])), 'DUPLICATE_ID');
    expectOpError(add(makeUnion('bad', [C])), 'INVALID_ID');
  });
});

describe('updateUnion', () => {
  it('changes status, order and partners; the inverse restores them', () => {
    const tree = applyOp(family(), { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(D) }).tree;
    const op: Op = {
      type: 'updateUnion',
      payload: { id: U, changes: { status: 'widowed', order: 2, partnerIds: [A, D] } },
    };
    const result = applyOp(tree, op);
    expect(result.tree.unions[U]).toEqual(makeUnion(U, [A, D], { status: 'widowed', order: 2 }));
    expect(result.inverse).toEqual({
      type: 'updateUnion',
      payload: { id: U, changes: { partnerIds: [A, B], order: 1, status: 'married' } },
    });
    roundTrip(tree, op);
  });

  it("refuses a child becoming a partner in their own parents' union", () => {
    expectOpError(
      () =>
        applyOp(family(), {
          type: 'updateUnion',
          payload: { id: U, changes: { partnerIds: [A, C] } },
        }),
      'CYCLE',
    );
  });

  it('refuses bad partners and a missing union', () => {
    expectOpError(
      () =>
        applyOp(family(), {
          type: 'updateUnion',
          payload: { id: U, changes: { partnerIds: [A, A] } },
        }),
      'INVALID_PARTNERS',
    );
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'updateUnion', payload: { id: V, changes: { order: 2 } } }),
      'UNION_NOT_FOUND',
    );
  });
});

describe('deleteUnion and restoreUnion', () => {
  it('deletes a childless union; the inverse restores it', () => {
    const tree = applyOp(family(), { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(V, [C]) }).tree;
    const op: Op = { type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: V } };
    expect(applyOp(tree, op).inverse).toEqual({
      type: 'restoreUnion',
      payload: makeUnion(V, [C]),
    });
    roundTrip(tree, op);
  });

  it('refuses a union with children, a missing union and a used id', () => {
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: U } }),
      'UNION_HAS_CHILDREN',
    );
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: V } }),
      'UNION_NOT_FOUND',
    );
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'restoreUnion', payload: makeUnion(U, [A]) }),
      'DUPLICATE_ID',
    );
  });
});

describe('setParents', () => {
  it('moves and detaches a child; the inverse puts them back', () => {
    const tree = applyOp(family(), { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(V, [A]) }).tree;
    const moved = roundTrip(tree, {
      type: 'setParents',
      payload: { personId: C, parentUnionId: V },
    });
    expect(moved.persons[C]?.parentUnionId).toBe(V);
    const detached = roundTrip(tree, {
      type: 'setParents',
      payload: { personId: C, parentUnionId: null },
    });
    expect(detached.persons[C]).toEqual(makePerson(C));
  });

  it('refuses to make someone their own ancestor', () => {
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'setParents', payload: { personId: A, parentUnionId: U } }),
      'CYCLE',
    );
    // C has a union V with a son D. A cannot become the child of his own son C.
    let tree = applyOp(family(), { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(V, [C]) }).tree;
    tree = applyOp(tree, { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(D, { parentUnionId: V }) }).tree;
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(tree, { type: 'setParents', payload: { personId: A, parentUnionId: V } }),
      'CYCLE',
    );
    expect(isOwnAncestor(tree, A)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOwnAncestor(tree, D)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('needs an existing person and union', () => {
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'setParents', payload: { personId: D, parentUnionId: U } }),
      'PERSON_NOT_FOUND',
    );
    expectOpError(
      () => applyOp(family(), { type: 'setParents', payload: { personId: C, parentUnionId: V } }),
      'UNION_NOT_FOUND',
    );
  });
});

describe('restorePerson', () => {
  // D (female) is the daughter of union V (partner E) and C's wife in union W (no children).
  function withInLaw(): TreeState {
    let tree = family();
    tree = applyOp(tree, { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(E) }).tree;
    tree = applyOp(tree, { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(V, [E]) }).tree;
    tree = applyOp(tree, {
      type: 'addPerson',
      payload: makePerson(D, { gender: 'female', parentUnionId: V }),
    }).tree;
    return applyOp(tree, { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(W, [C, D]) }).tree;
  }

  it('puts back the person and their union exactly', () => {
    const tree = withInLaw();
    roundTrip(tree, { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: D } });
    expect(applyOp(tree, { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: D } }).inverse).toEqual({
      type: 'restorePerson',
      payload: { person: tree.persons[D], unions: [tree.unions[W]] },
    });
  });

  it('refuses when a union it puts back has a new partner', () => {
    const { tree, inverse } = applyOp(withInLaw(), { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: D } });
    const changed = applyOp(tree, {
      type: 'updateUnion',
      payload: { id: W, changes: { partnerIds: [E] } },
    }).tree;
    expectOpError(() => applyOp(changed, inverse), 'RESTORE_CONFLICT');
  });

  it('refuses when the restored person would become their own ancestor', () => {
    const { tree: deleted, inverse } = applyOp(withInLaw(), {
      type: 'deletePerson',
      payload: { id: D },
    });
    // K is born to W (now only C), then K becomes the only partner of V, D's parents' union.
    let tree = applyOp(deleted, {
      type: 'addPerson',
      payload: makePerson(K, { parentUnionId: W }),
    }).tree;
    tree = applyOp(tree, {
      type: 'updateUnion',
      payload: { id: V, changes: { partnerIds: [K] } },
    }).tree;
    expectOpError(() => applyOp(tree, inverse), 'CYCLE');
  });

  it('checks the snapshot', () => {
    const restore = (person: Person, unions: Union[]) => () =>
      applyOp(family(), { type: 'restorePerson', payload: { person, unions } });
    expectOpError(restore(makePerson(A), []), 'DUPLICATE_ID');
    expectOpError(restore(makePerson(D), [makeUnion(V, [C])]), 'INVALID_PARTNERS');
    expectOpError(restore(makePerson(D), [makeUnion(V, [D, E])]), 'PERSON_NOT_FOUND');
    expectOpError(restore(makePerson(D, { parentUnionId: V }), []), 'UNION_NOT_FOUND');
    expectOpError(restore(makePerson(D), [makeUnion(A, [D])]), 'DUPLICATE_ID');
  });
});

describe('setProgenitor', () => {
  it('chooses or clears the progenitor; the inverse restores the previous one', () => {
    const choose = (personId: string | null): Op => ({ type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId } });
    expect(roundTrip(family(), choose(C)).progenitorId).toBe(C);
    expect(roundTrip(family(), choose(null)).progenitorId).toBeNull();
    expect(applyOp(family(), choose(C)).inverse).toEqual(choose(A));
    expectOpError(() => applyOp(family(), choose(D)), 'PERSON_NOT_FOUND');
  });
});

describe('batch', () => {
  const addSpouse: Op = {
    type: 'batch',
    payload: {
      ops: [
        { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(D, { gender: 'female' }) },
        { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(V, [C, D]) },
      ],
    },
  };

  it('applies ops in order; the inverse undoes them in reverse order', () => {
    const result = applyOp(family(), addSpouse);
    expect(result.tree.unions[V]?.partnerIds).toEqual([C, D]);
    expect(result.inverse).toEqual({
      type: 'batch',
      payload: {
        ops: [
          { type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: V } },
          { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: D } },
        ],
      },
    });
    roundTrip(family(), addSpouse);
  });

  it('fails as a whole and leaves the tree untouched', () => {
    const tree = family();
    const before = structuredClone(tree);
    expectOpError(
      () =>
        applyOp(tree, {
          type: 'batch',
          payload: {
            ops: [
              { type: 'addPerson', payload: makePerson(D) },
              { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: A } },
            ],
          },
        }),
      'PROGENITOR_PROTECTED',
    );
    expect(tree).toEqual(before);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `applyOp`**

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/apply-op.ts`:
```ts
import { isObjectId } from './ids.ts';
import { OpError } from './op-error.ts';
import type { Op, SingleOp } from './ops.ts';
import {
  MAX_PERSONS,
  type Person,
  type PersonChanges,
  type Union,
  type UnionChanges,
} from './schemas.ts';
import type { TreeState } from './state.ts';

export interface ApplyOpResult {
  tree: TreeState;
  /** The op that undoes this one (spec §6.1). */
  inverse: Op;
}

interface SingleResult {
  tree: TreeState;
  inverse: SingleOp;
}

function withEntry<T>(record: Readonly<Record<string, T>>, id: string, value: T): Record<string, T> {
  return { ...record, [id]: value };
}

function withoutEntry<T>(record: Readonly<Record<string, T>>, id: string): Record<string, T> {
  const copy = { ...record };
  delete copy[id];
  return copy;
}

function getPerson(tree: TreeState, id: string): Person {
  const person = tree.persons[id];
  if (!person) throw new OpError('PERSON_NOT_FOUND', `Person ${id} does not exist`);
  return person;
}

function getUnion(tree: TreeState, id: string): Union {
  const union = tree.unions[id];
  if (!union) throw new OpError('UNION_NOT_FOUND', `Union ${id} does not exist`);
  return union;
}

/** Clients generate ids (spec §6.1), so they are checked like any other input. */
function assertNewId(tree: TreeState, id: string): void {
  if (!isObjectId(id)) throw new OpError('INVALID_ID', `"${id}" is not an ObjectId`);
  if (Object.hasOwn(tree.persons, id) || Object.hasOwn(tree.unions, id)) {
    throw new OpError('DUPLICATE_ID', `Id ${id} is already used`);
  }
}

function assertRoomForPerson(tree: TreeState): void {
  if (Object.keys(tree.persons).length >= MAX_PERSONS) throw new OpError('TREE_FULL');
}

function assertDistinctPartners(partnerIds: readonly string[]): void {
  if (
    partnerIds.length < 1 ||
    partnerIds.length > 2 ||
    new Set(partnerIds).size !== partnerIds.length
  ) {
    throw new OpError('INVALID_PARTNERS');
  }
}

function assertPartners(tree: TreeState, partnerIds: readonly string[]): void {
  assertDistinctPartners(partnerIds);
  for (const partnerId of partnerIds) getPerson(tree, partnerId);
}

/** True when someone was born into one of these unions. One pass over the people. */
function anyChildOf(tree: TreeState, unionIds: ReadonlySet<string>): boolean {
  for (const person of Object.values(tree.persons)) {
    if (person.parentUnionId !== undefined && unionIds.has(person.parentUnionId)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * True when the person is their own ancestor: walking up from their parents' union, through
 * each partner's parents' union, reaches a union they are a partner of. Visits each union once.
 */
export function isOwnAncestor(tree: TreeState, personId: string): boolean {
  const start = tree.persons[personId]?.parentUnionId;
  if (start === undefined) return false;
  const queue: string[] = [start];
  const seen = new Set<string>(queue);
  for (let index = 0; index < queue.length; index += 1) {
    const union = tree.unions[queue[index] as string];
    for (const partnerId of union?.partnerIds ?? []) {
      if (partnerId === personId) return true;
      const parent = tree.persons[partnerId]?.parentUnionId;
      if (parent !== undefined && !seen.has(parent)) {
        seen.add(parent);
        queue.push(parent);
      }
    }
  }
  return false;
}

function assertNoCycle(tree: TreeState, personIds: readonly string[]): void {
  for (const personId of personIds) {
    if (isOwnAncestor(tree, personId)) {
      throw new OpError('CYCLE', `Person ${personId} would become their own ancestor`);
    }
  }
}

function addPerson(tree: TreeState, person: Person): SingleResult {
  assertNewId(tree, person.id);
  assertRoomForPerson(tree);
  if (person.parentUnionId !== undefined) getUnion(tree, person.parentUnionId);
  return {
    tree: { ...tree, persons: withEntry(tree.persons, person.id, person) },
    inverse: { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: person.id } },
  };
}

function updatePerson(tree: TreeState, id: string, changes: PersonChanges): SingleResult {
  const person = getPerson(tree, id);
  const current = person as Record<string, unknown>;
  const next: Record<string, unknown> = { ...person };
  const previous: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const [field, value] of Object.entries(changes)) {
    if (value === undefined) continue;
    // null clears a field; an absent old value goes back as null for the same reason.
    previous[field] = current[field] ?? null;
    if (value === null) delete next[field];
    else next[field] = value;
  }
  return {
    tree: { ...tree, persons: withEntry(tree.persons, id, next as Person) },
    inverse: { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id, changes: previous as PersonChanges } },
  };
}

function deletePerson(tree: TreeState, id: string): SingleResult {
  const person = getPerson(tree, id);
  if (tree.progenitorId === id) throw new OpError('PROGENITOR_PROTECTED');
  const own = Object.values(tree.unions).filter((union) => union.partnerIds.includes(id));
  if (anyChildOf(tree, new Set(own.map((union) => union.id)))) {
    throw new OpError('PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN');
  }
  const unions = { ...tree.unions };
  for (const union of own) {
    if (union.partnerIds.length === 1) delete unions[union.id];
    else unions[union.id] = { ...union, partnerIds: union.partnerIds.filter((p) => p !== id) };
  }
  return {
    tree: { ...tree, persons: withoutEntry(tree.persons, id), unions },
    inverse: { type: 'restorePerson', payload: { person, unions: own } },
  };
}

/** addUnion and restoreUnion. A brand-new union has no children, so it cannot make a cycle. */
function addUnion(tree: TreeState, union: Union): SingleResult {
  assertNewId(tree, union.id);
  assertPartners(tree, union.partnerIds);
  return {
    tree: { ...tree, unions: withEntry(tree.unions, union.id, union) },
    inverse: { type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: union.id } },
  };
}

function updateUnion(tree: TreeState, id: string, changes: UnionChanges): SingleResult {
  const union = getUnion(tree, id);
  const next: Union = { ...union };
  const previous: UnionChanges = {};
  if (changes.partnerIds !== undefined) {
    assertPartners(tree, changes.partnerIds);
    previous.partnerIds = union.partnerIds;
    next.partnerIds = changes.partnerIds;
  }
  if (changes.order !== undefined) {
    previous.order = union.order;
    next.order = changes.order;
  }
  if (changes.status !== undefined) {
    previous.status = union.status;
    next.status = changes.status;
  }
  const nextTree: TreeState = { ...tree, unions: withEntry(tree.unions, id, next) };
  if (changes.partnerIds !== undefined) assertNoCycle(nextTree, changes.partnerIds);
  return { tree: nextTree, inverse: { type: 'updateUnion', payload: { id, changes: previous } } };
}

function deleteUnion(tree: TreeState, id: string): SingleResult {
  const union = getUnion(tree, id);
  if (anyChildOf(tree, new Set([id]))) throw new OpError('UNION_HAS_CHILDREN');
  return {
    tree: { ...tree, unions: withoutEntry(tree.unions, id) },
    inverse: { type: 'restoreUnion', payload: union },
  };
}

function setParents(tree: TreeState, personId: string, parentUnionId: string | null): SingleResult {
  const person = getPerson(tree, personId);
  if (parentUnionId !== null) getUnion(tree, parentUnionId);
  const { parentUnionId: previous, ...rest } = person;
  const nextPerson: Person = parentUnionId === null ? rest : { ...rest, parentUnionId };
  const nextTree: TreeState = { ...tree, persons: withEntry(tree.persons, personId, nextPerson) };
  if (parentUnionId !== null) assertNoCycle(nextTree, [personId]);
  return {
    tree: nextTree,
    inverse: { type: 'setParents', payload: { personId, parentUnionId: previous ?? null } },
  };
}

function restorePerson(tree: TreeState, person: Person, snapshots: readonly Union[]): SingleResult {
  assertNewId(tree, person.id);
  assertRoomForPerson(tree);
  const unions = { ...tree.unions };
  for (const snapshot of snapshots) {
    assertDistinctPartners(snapshot.partnerIds);
    if (!snapshot.partnerIds.includes(person.id)) {
      throw new OpError('INVALID_PARTNERS', `Union ${snapshot.id} does not include the person`);
    }
    for (const partnerId of snapshot.partnerIds) {
      if (partnerId !== person.id) getPerson(tree, partnerId);
    }
    const current = tree.unions[snapshot.id];
    if (current) {
      // The union kept the other partner when the person was deleted; it may not have gained
      // anyone else since.
      if (!current.partnerIds.every((partnerId) => snapshot.partnerIds.includes(partnerId))) {
        throw new OpError('RESTORE_CONFLICT', `Union ${snapshot.id} has changed partners`);
      }
    } else {
      assertNewId(tree, snapshot.id);
    }
    unions[snapshot.id] = snapshot;
  }
  const nextTree: TreeState = {
    ...tree,
    persons: withEntry(tree.persons, person.id, person),
    unions,
  };
  if (person.parentUnionId !== undefined) getUnion(nextTree, person.parentUnionId);
  assertNoCycle(nextTree, [person.id]);
  return { tree: nextTree, inverse: { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: person.id } } };
}

function setProgenitor(tree: TreeState, personId: string | null): SingleResult {
  if (personId !== null) getPerson(tree, personId);
  return {
    tree: { ...tree, progenitorId: personId },
    inverse: { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: tree.progenitorId } },
  };
}

function applySingle(tree: TreeState, op: SingleOp): SingleResult {
  switch (op.type) {
    case 'addPerson':
      return addPerson(tree, op.payload);
    case 'updatePerson':
      return updatePerson(tree, op.payload.id, op.payload.changes);
    case 'deletePerson':
      return deletePerson(tree, op.payload.id);
    case 'addUnion':
    case 'restoreUnion':
      return addUnion(tree, op.payload);
    case 'updateUnion':
      return updateUnion(tree, op.payload.id, op.payload.changes);
    case 'deleteUnion':
      return deleteUnion(tree, op.payload.id);
    case 'setParents':
      return setParents(tree, op.payload.personId, op.payload.parentUnionId);
    case 'restorePerson':
      return restorePerson(tree, op.payload.person, op.payload.unions);
    case 'setProgenitor':
      return setProgenitor(tree, op.payload.personId);
  }
}

/**
 * Applies one change without mutating the tree and returns the new tree plus the op that undoes
 * it. Throws OpError when a rule of spec §6.1 forbids the change. The browser (optimistic
 * updates) and the API (inside the write transaction) run this same function.
 */
export function applyOp(tree: TreeState, op: Op): ApplyOpResult {
  if (op.type !== 'batch') return applySingle(tree, op);
  let current = tree;
  const inverses: SingleOp[] = [];
  for (const child of op.payload.ops) {
    const result = applySingle(current, child);
    current = result.tree;
    inverses.push(result.inverse);
  }
  return { tree: current, inverse: { type: 'batch', payload: { ops: inverses.reverse() } } };
}
```

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/apply-op.properties.test.ts`:
```ts
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { applyOp, type ApplyOpResult } from './apply-op.ts';
import { OpError } from './op-error.ts';
import type { Op, SingleOp } from './ops.ts';
import { EMPTY_TREE, type TreeState } from './state.ts';
import { invariantViolations, makePerson, makeUnion, testId } from './testing.ts';

/** Indexes pick an existing person or union (modulo the count), so most commands apply. */
type Command =
  | { kind: 'addPerson'; parent: number | null }
  | { kind: 'addUnion'; a: number; b: number | null }
  | { kind: 'updatePerson'; target: number; name: string; clearBio: boolean }
  | { kind: 'updateUnion'; target: number; a: number; b: number | null }
  | { kind: 'deletePerson'; target: number }
  | { kind: 'deleteUnion'; target: number }
  | { kind: 'setParents'; target: number; union: number | null }
  | { kind: 'setProgenitor'; target: number | null };

const index = fc.nat({ max: 50 });
const command: fc.Arbitrary<Command> = fc.oneof(
  fc.record({ kind: fc.constant('addPerson' as const), parent: fc.option(index) }),
  fc.record({ kind: fc.constant('addUnion' as const), a: index, b: fc.option(index) }),
  fc.record({
    kind: fc.constant('updatePerson' as const),
    target: index,
    name: fc.string({ minLength: 1, maxLength: 8 }),
    clearBio: fc.boolean(),
  }),
  fc.record({
    kind: fc.constant('updateUnion' as const),
    target: index,
    a: index,
    b: fc.option(index),
  }),
  fc.record({ kind: fc.constant('deletePerson' as const), target: index }),
  fc.record({ kind: fc.constant('deleteUnion' as const), target: index }),
  fc.record({ kind: fc.constant('setParents' as const), target: index, union: fc.option(index) }),
  fc.record({ kind: fc.constant('setProgenitor' as const), target: fc.option(index) }),
);

function pick(ids: readonly string[], at: number): string | undefined {
  return ids.length === 0 ? undefined : ids[at % ids.length];
}

function partners(a: string, b: string | undefined): string[] {
  return b !== undefined && b !== a ? [a, b] : [a];
}

/** Turns a command into an op on this tree, or null when there is nothing to act on. */
function toOp(tree: TreeState, cmd: Command, nextId: () => string): SingleOp | null {
  const people = Object.keys(tree.persons).sort();
  const unions = Object.keys(tree.unions).sort();
  switch (cmd.kind) {
    case 'addPerson': {
      const parent = cmd.parent === null ? undefined : pick(unions, cmd.parent);
      const person = makePerson(nextId(), parent === undefined ? {} : { parentUnionId: parent });
      return { type: 'addPerson', payload: person };
    }
    case 'addUnion': {
      const a = pick(people, cmd.a);
      if (a === undefined) return null;
      const b = cmd.b === null ? undefined : pick(people, cmd.b);
      return { type: 'addUnion', payload: makeUnion(nextId(), partners(a, b)) };
    }
    case 'updatePerson': {
      const id = pick(people, cmd.target);
      if (id === undefined) return null;
      const changes = cmd.clearBio
        ? { bio: null }
        : { fullName: cmd.name.trim() || 'x', bio: cmd.name };
      return { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id, changes } };
    }
    case 'updateUnion': {
      const id = pick(unions, cmd.target);
      const a = pick(people, cmd.a);
      if (id === undefined || a === undefined) return null;
      const b = cmd.b === null ? undefined : pick(people, cmd.b);
      return {
        type: 'updateUnion',
        payload: { id, changes: { partnerIds: partners(a, b), status: 'divorced' } },
      };
    }
    case 'deletePerson': {
      const id = pick(people, cmd.target);
      return id === undefined ? null : { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id } };
    }
    case 'deleteUnion': {
      const id = pick(unions, cmd.target);
      return id === undefined ? null : { type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id } };
    }
    case 'setParents': {
      const personId = pick(people, cmd.target);
      if (personId === undefined) return null;
      const parentUnionId = cmd.union === null ? null : (pick(unions, cmd.union) ?? null);
      return { type: 'setParents', payload: { personId, parentUnionId } };
    }
    case 'setProgenitor': {
      const personId = cmd.target === null ? null : (pick(people, cmd.target) ?? null);
      return { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId } };
    }
  }
}

function tryApply(tree: TreeState, op: Op): ApplyOpResult | null {
  try {
    return applyOp(tree, op);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof OpError) return null;
    throw error;
  }
}

/** Applies op; checks immutability, invariants and the inverse round trip on success. */
function step(tree: TreeState, op: Op): TreeState {
  const before = structuredClone(tree);
  const result = tryApply(tree, op);
  expect(tree).toEqual(before);
  if (!result) return tree;
  expect(invariantViolations(result.tree)).toEqual([]);
  expect(applyOp(result.tree, result.inverse).tree).toEqual(before);
  return result.tree;
}

function idMaker(): () => string {
  let counter = 0;
  return () => {
    counter += 1;
    return testId(counter);
  };
}

describe('applyOp properties', () => {
  it('keeps the tree valid, never mutates it, and every inverse restores it exactly', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(fc.array(command, { maxLength: 60 }), (commands) => {
        const nextId = idMaker();
        let tree = EMPTY_TREE;
        for (const cmd of commands) {
          const op = toOp(tree, cmd, nextId);
          if (op) tree = step(tree, op);
        }
      }),
      { numRuns: 300 },
    );
  });

  it('round-trips batches', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.array(command, { maxLength: 30 }),
        fc.array(fc.array(command, { minLength: 1, maxLength: 4 }), { maxLength: 8 }),
        (setup, groups) => {
          const nextId = idMaker();
          let tree = EMPTY_TREE;
          for (const cmd of setup) {
            const op = toOp(tree, cmd, nextId);
            if (op) tree = step(tree, op);
          }
          for (const group of groups) {
            // Build each child op against the tree as the batch will see it.
            const ops: SingleOp[] = [];
            let preview = tree;
            for (const cmd of group) {
              const op = toOp(preview, cmd, nextId);
              const result = op ? tryApply(preview, op) : null;
              if (op && result) {
                ops.push(op);
                preview = result.tree;
              }
            }
            if (ops.length > 0) tree = step(tree, { type: 'batch', payload: { ops } });
          }
        },
      ),
      { numRuns: 200 },
    );
  });
});
```

Run: `pnpm vitest run packages/shared/src/tree`
Expected: PASS. If a property fails, fast-check prints the shrunk command list; fix `applyOp` unless the counterexample shows a mistake in the test.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `packages/shared/src/tree/lineage.ts`:
```ts
import type { Person, Union } from './schemas.ts';
import type { TreeState } from './state.ts';

/** Spec §8: derived, never stored. */
export type Lineage = 'blood' | 'in-law' | 'unlinked';

export interface TreeIndex {
  /** The person's unions by `order`, then id. */
  unionsOf(personId: string): readonly Union[];
  /** The union's children by `siblingOrder` (people without one last), then id. */
  childrenOf(unionId: string): readonly Person[];
}

const NONE: readonly never[] = [];

function byOrder(a: Union, b: Union): number {
  return a.order - b.order || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0);
}

function bySiblingOrder(a: Person, b: Person): number {
  const orderA = a.siblingOrder ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  const orderB = b.siblingOrder ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  if (orderA !== orderB) return orderA < orderB ? -1 : 1;
  return a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0;
}

function push<T>(map: Map<string, T[]>, key: string, value: T): void {
  const list = map.get(key);
  if (list) list.push(value);
  else map.set(key, [value]);
}

/** Lookups from people to their unions and from unions to their children. */
export function buildTreeIndex(tree: TreeState): TreeIndex {
  const unionsByPerson = new Map<string, Union[]>();
  for (const union of Object.values(tree.unions)) {
    for (const partnerId of union.partnerIds) push(unionsByPerson, partnerId, union);
  }
  const childrenByUnion = new Map<string, Person[]>();
  for (const person of Object.values(tree.persons)) {
    if (person.parentUnionId !== undefined) push(childrenByUnion, person.parentUnionId, person);
  }
  for (const list of unionsByPerson.values()) list.sort(byOrder);
  for (const list of childrenByUnion.values()) list.sort(bySiblingOrder);
  return {
    unionsOf: (personId) => unionsByPerson.get(personId) ?? NONE,
    childrenOf: (unionId) => childrenByUnion.get(unionId) ?? NONE,
  };
}

interface Walk {
  generations: Map<string, number>;
  blood: Set<string>;
}

/**
 * One breadth-first walk down from the progenitor. Blood members are queued; their partners get
 * the same generation but are never walked (in-laws do not expand, spec §8). Descent wins over
 * marriage when someone is both.
 */
function walk(tree: TreeState, index: TreeIndex): Walk {
  const generations = new Map<string, number>();
  const blood = new Set<string>();
  const root = tree.progenitorId;
  if (root === null || !tree.persons[root]) return { generations, blood };
  const queue = [root];
  blood.add(root);
  generations.set(root, 1);
  for (let at = 0; at < queue.length; at += 1) {
    const id = queue[at] as string;
    const generation = generations.get(id) as number;
    for (const union of index.unionsOf(id)) {
      for (const partnerId of union.partnerIds) {
        if (!generations.has(partnerId)) generations.set(partnerId, generation);
      }
      for (const child of index.childrenOf(union.id)) {
        if (blood.has(child.id)) continue;
        blood.add(child.id);
        generations.set(child.id, generation + 1);
        queue.push(child.id);
      }
    }
  }
  return { generations, blood };
}

/** Spec §5: the progenitor is generation 1; people not linked to them are absent. */
export function computeGenerations(
  tree: TreeState,
  index: TreeIndex = buildTreeIndex(tree),
): ReadonlyMap<string, number> {
  return walk(tree, index).generations;
}

/** Spec §8: one entry per person. */
export function classifyLineage(
  tree: TreeState,
  index: TreeIndex = buildTreeIndex(tree),
): ReadonlyMap<string, Lineage> {
  const { generations, blood } = walk(tree, index);
  const lineage = new Map<string, Lineage>();
  for (const id of Object.keys(tree.persons)) {
    lineage.set(id, blood.has(id) ? 'blood' : generations.has(id) ? 'in-law' : 'unlinked');
  }
  return lineage;
}
```

```ts
  buildTreeIndex,
  classifyLineage,
  computeGenerations,
  type Lineage,
  type TreeIndex,
} from './lineage.ts';
```

```ts
import { ERROR_CODES, type ErrorCode, type ErrorDetail } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import type { OpErrorCode } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';

export class AppError extends Error {
  readonly code: ErrorCode;
  readonly status: number;
  readonly details: ErrorDetail[] | undefined;
  /** Only with RULE_VIOLATION: the rule applyOp refused. */
  readonly reason: OpErrorCode | undefined;

  constructor(code: ErrorCode, message: string, details?: ErrorDetail[], reason?: OpErrorCode) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'AppError';
    this.code = code;
    this.status = ERROR_CODES[code].status;
    this.details = details;
    this.reason = reason;
  }
}
```

```ts
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly code: ClientErrorCode;
  readonly status: number;
  readonly details: ErrorDetail[] | undefined;
  /** Only with RULE_VIOLATION: the rule applyOp refused (translated as `tree.rules.<reason>`). */
  readonly reason: OpErrorCode | undefined;

  constructor(
    code: ClientErrorCode,
    message: string,
    status: number,
    details?: ErrorDetail[],
    reason?: OpErrorCode,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.code = code;
    this.status = status;
    this.details = details;
    this.reason = reason;
  }
}
```
and `CODE_BY_STATUS` with:
```ts
const CODE_BY_STATUS: Partial<Record<number, ErrorCode>> = {
  400: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
  401: 'UNAUTHENTICATED',
  403: 'FORBIDDEN',
  404: 'NOT_FOUND',
  // A newer API may send a reason this client does not know; the status still tells the store
  // whether to reload (409) or roll back (422).
  409: 'VERSION_CONFLICT',
  422: 'RULE_VIOLATION',
  429: 'RATE_LIMITED',
};
```

### Task 5: Person, Union and Operation models; load and save a tree

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/models/Person.ts`, `apps/api/src/models/Union.ts`, `apps/api/src/models/Operation.ts`, `apps/api/src/tree/dto.ts`, `apps/api/src/tree/store.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/models/Clan.ts`, `apps/api/src/db/indexes.ts`
- Test: `apps/api/src/tree/dto.test.ts`, `apps/api/src/tree/store.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Person`, `Union`, `TreeState`, `treeStateFromLists`, `applyOp`, `newObjectId`, `CLEARABLE_PERSON_FIELDS` from `@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`; `AppError`.
- Produces:
  - Models `PersonModel` (`'Person'`), `UnionModel` (`'Union'`), `OperationModel` (`'Operation'`, unique `{ clanId: 1, seq: 1 }`), all with an explicit `_id` (client-generated ObjectIds) and a `clanId` index; all in `MODELS`.
  - `Clan` gains `version: number` (default 0) and `progenitorId: ObjectId | null` (default null).
  - `apps/api/src/tree/dto.ts`: `interface PersonRecord`, `interface UnionRecord`, `toPerson(record): Person`, `toUnion(record): Union`, `personWrite(clanId, person)`, `unionWrite(clanId, union)` (upsert `updateOne` bulk operations).
  - `apps/api/src/tree/store.ts`: `findClan(clanId: string, session: ClientSession): Promise<ClanDocument>` (throws `NOT_FOUND`), `loadTreeState(clan: ClanDocument, session: ClientSession): Promise<TreeState>`, `diffRecords<T>(before, after): { changed: T[]; removed: string[] }`, `saveTreeChanges(clanId: string, before: TreeState, after: TreeState, session: ClientSession): Promise<void>`.

Why the store compares references: `applyOp` copies only the records it changes (Task 2), so `before.persons[id] !== after.persons[id]` is exactly "this document changed". One op touches a handful of documents even in a 5,000-person tree.
