Create `apps/api/src/tree/store.test.ts`:
```ts
import {
  applyOp,
  EMPTY_TREE,
  newObjectId,
  type Op,
  type Person,
  type TreeState,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import mongoose, { Types, type ClientSession } from 'mongoose';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { Clan } from '../models/Clan.ts';
import { PersonModel } from '../models/Person.ts';
import { UnionModel } from '../models/Union.ts';
import { useTestDatabase } from '../test/database.ts';
import { diffRecords, findClan, loadTreeState, saveTreeChanges } from './store.ts';

useTestDatabase();

function person(overrides: Partial<Person> = {}): Person {
  return {
    id: newObjectId(),
    fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư',
    aliases: [],
    gender: 'male',
    death: { isDeceased: false },
    isAdopted: false,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

async function newClan() {
  return Clan.create({ name: 'Họ Nguyễn', ownerId: new Types.ObjectId() });
}

function inTransaction<T>(fn: (session: ClientSession) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  return mongoose.connection.transaction(fn);
}

/** Applies ops one after another to a tree. */
function applyAll(tree: TreeState, ops: Op[]): TreeState {
  return ops.reduce((current, op) => applyOp(current, op).tree, tree);
}

describe('Clan tree fields', () => {
  it('start at version 0 without a progenitor', async () => {
    const clan = await newClan();
    expect(clan.version).toBe(0);
    expect(clan.progenitorId).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('diffRecords', () => {
  it('finds changed objects by reference and removed ids', () => {
    const a = { n: 1 };
    const b = { n: 2 };
    expect(diffRecords({ a, b }, { a, b: { n: 2 }, c: { n: 3 } })).toEqual({
      changed: [{ n: 2 }, { n: 3 }],
      removed: [],
    });
    expect(diffRecords({ a, b }, { a })).toEqual({ changed: [], removed: ['b'] });
  });
});

describe('tree store', () => {
  it('saves a tree and loads it back exactly', async () => {
    const clan = await newClan();
    const father = person({
      birth: { source: 'lunar', lunar: { day: 15, month: 10, year: 1931, isLeap: false } },
      death: { isDeceased: true, date: { source: 'approx', approxYear: 1990 } },
      branch: 'Chi trưởng',
      birthFamily: { surname: 'Nguyễn', hometown: 'Nam Định' },
      bio: 'Trưởng họ',
      burialPlace: 'Nghĩa trang làng',
      aliases: ['Cụ Tư'],
    });
    const unionId = newObjectId();
    const child = person({ fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Năm', siblingOrder: 1 });
    const after = applyAll(EMPTY_TREE, [
      { type: 'addPerson', payload: father },
      { type: 'addUnion', payload: { id: unionId, partnerIds: [father.id], order: 1, status: 'married' } },
      { type: 'addPerson', payload: { ...child, parentUnionId: unionId } },
      { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: father.id } },
    ]);

    await inTransaction((session) => saveTreeChanges(clan.id as string, EMPTY_TREE, after, session));
    clan.progenitorId = new Types.ObjectId(father.id);
    await clan.save();

    const loaded = await inTransaction(async (session) =>
      loadTreeState(await findClan(clan.id as string, session), session),
    );
    expect(loaded).toEqual(after);
  });

  it('updates, clears and deletes only what changed', async () => {
    const clan = await newClan();
    const kept = person({ fullName: 'Người giữ nguyên' });
    const edited = person({ bio: 'Sẽ bị xoá' });
    const removed = person();
    const first = applyAll(EMPTY_TREE, [
      { type: 'addPerson', payload: kept },
      { type: 'addPerson', payload: edited },
      { type: 'addPerson', payload: removed },
    ]);
    await inTransaction((session) => saveTreeChanges(clan.id as string, EMPTY_TREE, first, session));
    const keptBefore = await PersonModel.findById(kept.id).lean();

    const second = applyAll(first, [
      { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: edited.id, changes: { bio: null, fullName: 'Đã sửa' } } },
      { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: removed.id } },
    ]);
    await inTransaction((session) => saveTreeChanges(clan.id as string, first, second, session));

    expect(await PersonModel.countDocuments()).toBe(2);
    const editedDoc = await PersonModel.findById(edited.id).lean();
    expect(editedDoc?.fullName).toBe('Đã sửa');
    expect(editedDoc && 'bio' in editedDoc).toBe(false);
    // The untouched document was not written again.
    expect((await PersonModel.findById(kept.id).lean())?.updatedAt).toEqual(keptBefore?.updatedAt);
  });

  it('loads only the people and unions of the requested family tree', async () => {
    const mine = await newClan();
    const other = await newClan();
    const stranger = person();
    const tree = applyAll(EMPTY_TREE, [
      { type: 'addPerson', payload: stranger },
      { type: 'addUnion', payload: { id: newObjectId(), partnerIds: [stranger.id], order: 1, status: 'married' } },
    ]);
    await inTransaction((session) => saveTreeChanges(other.id as string, EMPTY_TREE, tree, session));

    const loaded = await inTransaction((session) => loadTreeState(mine, session));
    expect(loaded).toEqual(EMPTY_TREE);
    expect(await UnionModel.countDocuments({ clanId: other._id })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('says NOT_FOUND for a missing family tree', async () => {
    await expect(
      inTransaction((session) => findClan(new Types.ObjectId().toString(), session)),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOT_FOUND' });
  });
});
```

Create `apps/api/src/models/Person.ts`:
```ts
import { model, Schema } from 'mongoose';

/**
 * A person in a family tree (spec §5). `_id` is generated by the client (shared newObjectId) so
 * optimistic edits can show the person at once; applyOp has already validated every field.
 */
const personSchema = new Schema(
  {
    _id: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, required: true },
    clanId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Clan', required: true, index: true },
    fullName: { type: String, required: true },
    aliases: { type: [String], default: [] },
    gender: { type: String, enum: ['male', 'female'], required: true },
    birth: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed },
    death: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed, required: true },
    parentUnionId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Union' },
    isAdopted: { type: Boolean, required: true },
    siblingOrder: { type: Number },
    branch: { type: String },
    birthFamily: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed },
    bio: { type: String },
    burialPlace: { type: String },
  },
  { timestamps: true, minimize: false },
);

export const PersonModel = model('Person', personSchema);
```

Create `apps/api/src/models/Union.ts`:
```ts
import { model, Schema } from 'mongoose';

/** One marriage (spec §5); `_id` is generated by the client like Person ids. */
const unionSchema = new Schema(
  {
    _id: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, required: true },
    clanId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Clan', required: true, index: true },
    partnerIds: { type: [{ type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Person' }], required: true },
    order: { type: Number, required: true },
    status: { type: String, enum: ['married', 'divorced', 'widowed'], required: true },
  },
  { timestamps: true },
);

export const UnionModel = model('Union', unionSchema);
```

Create `apps/api/src/models/Operation.ts`:
```ts
import { model, Schema } from 'mongoose';

/**
 * The operation log (spec §5, §6.2): one entry per accepted change, numbered by the clan's
 * version. The unique (clanId, seq) index is the last guard against two writers taking one seq.
 */
const operationSchema = new Schema(
  {
    clanId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Clan', required: true },
    seq: { type: Number, required: true, min: 1 },
    userId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'User', required: true },
    type: { type: String, required: true },
    payload: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed, required: true },
    /** The op that undoes this one, as returned by applyOp (undo and history in P6). */
    inverse: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed, required: true },
  },
  { timestamps: { createdAt: true, updatedAt: false }, minimize: false },
);

operationSchema.index({ clanId: 1, seq: 1 }, { unique: true });

export const OperationModel = model('Operation', operationSchema);
```

```ts
    description: { type: String, default: '', trim: true, maxlength: 2000 },
    ownerId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'User', required: true },
    /** Bumped by every accepted operation (spec §6.2); equals the last Operation.seq. */

);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the DTOs and the store**

Create `apps/api/src/tree/dto.ts`:
```ts
import { CLEARABLE_PERSON_FIELDS, type Person, type Union } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { Types, type AnyBulkWriteOperation } from 'mongoose';

/** A Person document as `.lean()` returns it. */
export interface PersonRecord {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  fullName: string;
  aliases?: string[];
  gender: Person['gender'];
  birth?: Person['birth'] | null;
  death: Person['death'];
  parentUnionId?: Types.ObjectId | null;
  isAdopted: boolean;
  siblingOrder?: number | null;
  branch?: string | null;
  birthFamily?: Person['birthFamily'] | null;
  bio?: string | null;
  burialPlace?: string | null;
}

/** A Union document as `.lean()` returns it. */
export interface UnionRecord {
  _id: Types.ObjectId;
  partnerIds: Types.ObjectId[];
  order: number;
  status: Union['status'];
}

export type TreeWrite = AnyBulkWriteOperation<Record<string, unknown>>;

/** Optional fields are left out, never sent as null (the shared schemas use `.optional()`). */
export function toPerson(record: PersonRecord): Person {
  const person: Person = {
    id: record._id.toString(),
    fullName: record.fullName,
    aliases: record.aliases ?? [],
    gender: record.gender,
    death: record.death,
    isAdopted: record.isAdopted,
  };
  if (record.parentUnionId) person.parentUnionId = record.parentUnionId.toString();
  const optional = person as Record<string, unknown>;
  for (const field of CLEARABLE_PERSON_FIELDS) {
    const value = record[field];
    if (value !== undefined && value !== null) optional[field] = value;
  }
  return person;
}

export function toUnion(record: UnionRecord): Union {
  return {
    id: record._id.toString(),
    partnerIds: record.partnerIds.map((id) => id.toString()),
    order: record.order,
    status: record.status,
  };
}

/**
 * Insert or replace one person in this clan. Fields the person no longer has are unset. The
 * filter includes clanId, so an id used by another clan fails with a duplicate key (E11000).
 */
export function personWrite(clanId: Types.ObjectId, person: Person): TreeWrite {
  const { id, parentUnionId, ...fields } = person;
  const set: Record<string, unknown> = { ...fields };
  const unset: Record<string, ''> = {};
  if (parentUnionId === undefined) unset.parentUnionId = '';
  else set.parentUnionId = new Types.ObjectId(parentUnionId);
  for (const field of CLEARABLE_PERSON_FIELDS) {
    if (person[field] === undefined) unset[field] = '';
  }
  return {
    updateOne: {
      filter: { _id: new Types.ObjectId(id), clanId },
      update: { $set: set, ...(Object.keys(unset).length > 0 ? { $unset: unset } : {}) },
      upsert: true,
    },
  };
}

export function unionWrite(clanId: Types.ObjectId, union: Union): TreeWrite {
  return {
    updateOne: {
      filter: { _id: new Types.ObjectId(union.id), clanId },
      update: {
        $set: {
          partnerIds: union.partnerIds.map((id) => new Types.ObjectId(id)),
          order: union.order,
          status: union.status,
        },
      },
      upsert: true,
    },
  };
}
```

Create `apps/api/src/tree/store.ts`:
```ts
import { treeStateFromLists, type TreeState } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { Types, type ClientSession } from 'mongoose';
import { AppError } from '../http/AppError.ts';
import { Clan, type ClanDocument } from '../models/Clan.ts';
import { PersonModel } from '../models/Person.ts';
import { UnionModel } from '../models/Union.ts';
import {
  personWrite,
  toPerson,
  toUnion,
  unionWrite,
  type PersonRecord,
  type UnionRecord,
} from './dto.ts';

export async function findClan(clanId: string, session: ClientSession): Promise<ClanDocument> {
  const clan = await Clan.findById(clanId).session(session);
  if (!clan) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND', 'Family tree not found');
  return clan;
}

/** The whole tree of a clan. Call inside the transaction that also read the clan's version. */
export async function loadTreeState(clan: ClanDocument, session: ClientSession): Promise<TreeState> {
  // One after another: a session runs one operation at a time.
  const persons = await PersonModel.find({ clanId: clan._id })
    .sort({ _id: 1 })
    .session(session)
    .lean<PersonRecord[]>();
  const unions = await UnionModel.find({ clanId: clan._id })
    .sort({ _id: 1 })
    .session(session)
    .lean<UnionRecord[]>();
  return treeStateFromLists(
    clan.progenitorId ? clan.progenitorId.toString() : null,
    persons.map(toPerson),
    unions.map(toUnion),
  );
}

/** What applyOp changed: new or replaced objects (by reference) and removed ids. */
export function diffRecords<T>(
  before: Readonly<Record<string, T>>,
  after: Readonly<Record<string, T>>,
): { changed: T[]; removed: string[] } {
  const changed: T[] = [];
  for (const [id, value] of Object.entries(after)) {
    if (before[id] !== value) changed.push(value);
  }
  const removed = Object.keys(before).filter((id) => !Object.hasOwn(after, id));
  return { changed, removed };
}

/** Writes the documents that differ between two trees. The progenitor lives on the Clan. */
export async function saveTreeChanges(
  clanId: string,
  before: TreeState,
  after: TreeState,
  session: ClientSession,
): Promise<void> {
  const clan = new Types.ObjectId(clanId);
  const persons = diffRecords(before.persons, after.persons);
  const unions = diffRecords(before.unions, after.unions);
  if (persons.removed.length > 0) {
    await PersonModel.deleteMany({ clanId: clan, _id: { $in: persons.removed } }, { session });
  }
  if (unions.removed.length > 0) {
    await UnionModel.deleteMany({ clanId: clan, _id: { $in: unions.removed } }, { session });
  }
  if (persons.changed.length > 0) {
    await PersonModel.bulkWrite<Record<string, unknown>>(
      persons.changed.map((person) => personWrite(clan, person)),
      { session },
    );
  }
  if (unions.changed.length > 0) {
    await UnionModel.bulkWrite<Record<string, unknown>>(
      unions.changed.map((union) => unionWrite(clan, union)),
      { session },
    );
  }
}
```

If `tsc` rejects the `bulkWrite<Record<string, unknown>>` call, check the signature in `node_modules/mongoose/types/models.d.ts` (`bulkWrite<DocContents = TRawDocType>(writes: Array<AnyBulkWriteOperation<…>>, options)`) and keep `TreeWrite` and the generic argument the same type. `ClientSession` and `AnyBulkWriteOperation` are declared inside `declare module 'mongoose'` (`types/session.d.ts`, `types/models.d.ts`), so they import from `'mongoose'` like the other types.

### Task 6: Tree API — read the tree, submit ops, list ops

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/shared/src/api/tree.ts`, `apps/api/src/routes/tree.ts`
- Modify: `packages/shared/src/index.ts`, `apps/api/src/app.ts`
- Test: `packages/shared/src/api/tree.test.ts`, `apps/api/src/routes/tree.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `OpSchema`, `PersonSchema`, `UnionSchema`, `applyOp`, `needsOwner`, `OpError`, `Op` (`@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`; inside `packages/shared` via relative imports); `findClan`, `loadTreeState`, `saveTreeChanges` (Task 5); `OperationModel`; `AppError(code, message, details?, reason?)` (Task 4).
- Produces (`@ancestors-tree/shared`, root export):
  - `TreeResponseSchema` / `TreeResponse` = `{ version: number; progenitorId: string | null; persons: Person[]; unions: Union[] }`.
  - `SubmitOpRequestSchema` / `SubmitOpRequest` = `{ op: Op; baseVersion: number }`; `SubmitOpResponseSchema` / `SubmitOpResponse` = `{ seq: number }`.
  - `OPS_PAGE_SIZE = 500`; `OpsQuerySchema` (`since`, coerced, default 0); `OperationEntrySchema` / `OperationEntry` = `{ seq; userId; createdAt: string; op: Op }`; `OperationListSchema`.
- Produces (HTTP):
  - `GET /api/clans/:id/tree` (viewer+) → 200 `TreeResponse`, read in one snapshot transaction so `version` matches the data.
  - `POST /api/clans/:id/ops` (editor+; `setProgenitor`, also inside a batch, Owner only → 403 `FORBIDDEN`) with `SubmitOpRequest` → 200 `{ seq }`; 409 `VERSION_CONFLICT` when `baseVersion` is not the current version; 422 `RULE_VIOLATION` with `reason` when `applyOp` refuses (also `DUPLICATE_ID` when another family tree already uses the id); 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` for a malformed op.
  - `GET /api/clans/:id/ops?since=N` (viewer+) → 200 `OperationEntry[]` with `seq > N`, oldest first, at most 500.

The write transaction, in order: read the Clan (404 if gone) → compare `version` with `baseVersion` (409) → load the tree → `applyOp` (422) → write the changed Person/Union documents → insert the Operation with `seq = version + 1` and its inverse → set `Clan.version = seq` and `Clan.progenitorId`. `mongoose.connection.transaction()` retries the whole function on a transient write conflict; the retry then sees the new version and answers 409. The unique `(clanId, seq)` index turns any remaining race into a duplicate key, also answered with 409.

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { OpSchema } from '../tree/ops.ts';
import { PersonSchema, UnionSchema } from '../tree/schemas.ts';
import { IdSchema } from './common.ts';

/** GET /api/clans/:id/tree: the whole tree at one version. */
export const TreeResponseSchema = z.object({
  version: z.number().int().min(0),
  progenitorId: IdSchema.nullable(),
  persons: z.array(PersonSchema),
  unions: z.array(UnionSchema),
});

/** POST /api/clans/:id/ops: one op, made against `baseVersion` (spec §6.2). */
export const SubmitOpRequestSchema = z.object({
  op: OpSchema,
  baseVersion: z.number().int().min(0),
});

/** The op's sequence number, which is also the tree's new version. */
export const SubmitOpResponseSchema = z.object({ seq: z.number().int().min(1) });

/** Most entries one GET /ops call returns; ask again with the last seq for more. */
export const OPS_PAGE_SIZE = 500;

export const OpsQuerySchema = z.object({
  since: z.coerce.number().int().min(0).default(0),
});

export const OperationEntrySchema = z.object({
  seq: z.number().int().min(1),
  userId: IdSchema,
  createdAt: z.iso.datetime(),
  op: OpSchema,
});

export const OperationListSchema = z.array(OperationEntrySchema);

export type TreeResponse = z.infer<typeof TreeResponseSchema>;
export type SubmitOpRequest = z.infer<typeof SubmitOpRequestSchema>;
export type SubmitOpResponse = z.infer<typeof SubmitOpResponseSchema>;
export type OperationEntry = z.infer<typeof OperationEntrySchema>;
```

```ts
} from './api/tree.ts';
```

```ts
function person(overrides: Partial<Person> = {}): Person {
  return {
    id: newObjectId(),
    fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư',
    aliases: [],
    gender: 'male',
    death: { isDeceased: false },

}

function submit(agent: Agent, clanId: string, op: Op, baseVersion: number) {

}

/** An owner with a new family tree, plus an editor and a viewer of it. */

  const editor = await signUp(app);
  const viewer = await signUp(app);
  const clan = await createClan(owner.agent);
  await addMember(clan.id, editor.user.id, 'editor');
  await addMember(clan.id, viewer.user.id, 'viewer');

    const wife = person({ fullName: 'Lê Thị Hoa', gender: 'female' });
    const unionId = newObjectId();

    expect(tree.body.persons).toHaveLength(2);

    expect((await submit(editor.agent, clanId, update, 1)).status).toBe(200);
    const tree = await editor.agent.get(`/api/clans/${clanId}/tree`);
    expect(tree.body.persons).toEqual([{ ...person(), id: tu.id, fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư (Cụ)' }]);

    expect((await submit(editor.agent, clanId, remove, 2)).status).toBe(200);
    expect(await PersonModel.countDocuments({ clanId })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('answers 409 to a stale base version and changes nothing', async () => {
    const { editor, clanId } = await setup();
    await submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(person()), 0);
    const stale = await submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(person()), 0);
    expect(stale.status).toBe(409);
    expect(stale.body.error.code).toBe('VERSION_CONFLICT');
    expect(await PersonModel.countDocuments({ clanId })).toBe(1);
    expect((await Clan.findById(clanId))?.version).toBe(1);
  });

  it('lets only one of two simultaneous writers win', async () => {
    const { editor, owner, clanId } = await setup();
    const responses = await Promise.all([
      submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(person()), 0),
      submit(owner.agent, clanId, addPerson(person()), 0),
    ]);
    expect(responses.map((response) => response.status).sort()).toEqual([200, 409]);
    expect(await PersonModel.countDocuments({ clanId })).toBe(1);
    expect(await OperationModel.countDocuments({ clanId })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('answers 422 with the reason when a rule is broken, and changes nothing', async () => {
    const { owner, clanId } = await setup();
    const father = person();
    const unionId = newObjectId();
    const family: Op = {
      type: 'batch',
      payload: {
        ops: [
          addPerson(father),
          { type: 'addUnion', payload: { id: unionId, partnerIds: [father.id], order: 1, status: 'married' } },
          addPerson(person({ parentUnionId: unionId })),
        ],
      },
    };
    await submit(owner.agent, clanId, family, 0);
    const response = await submit(owner.agent, clanId, { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: father.id } }, 1);
    expect(response.status).toBe(422);
    expect(response.body.error).toMatchObject({
      code: 'RULE_VIOLATION',
      reason: 'PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN',
    });
    expect((await Clan.findById(clanId))?.version).toBe(1);
    expect(await PersonModel.countDocuments({ clanId })).toBe(2);
  });

  it('refuses an id that another family tree already uses', async () => {
    const { editor, clanId } = await setup();
    const other = await signUp(app);
    const otherClan = await createClan(other.agent, { name: 'Họ Trần' });
    const shared = person();
    expect((await submit(other.agent, otherClan.id, addPerson(shared), 0)).status).toBe(200);

    const response = await submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(shared), 0);
    expect(response.status).toBe(422);
    expect(response.body.error.reason).toBe('DUPLICATE_ID');
    expect((await Clan.findById(clanId))?.version).toBe(0);
  });

  it('rejects a malformed op with 400', async () => {
    const { editor, clanId } = await setup();
    const response = await submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(person({ id: 'not-an-id' })), 0);
    expect(response.status).toBe(400);
    expect(response.body.error.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
  });

  it('lets viewers read but not write', async () => {
    const { viewer, clanId } = await setup();
    expect((await submit(viewer.agent, clanId, addPerson(person()), 0)).status).toBe(403);
  });

  it('lets only the owner choose the progenitor, also inside a batch', async () => {
    const { owner, editor, clanId } = await setup();
    const tu = person();
    await submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(tu), 0);
    const choose: Op = { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: tu.id } };

    const denied = await submit(editor.agent, clanId, choose, 1);
    expect(denied.status).toBe(403);
    const hidden: Op = { type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [addPerson(person()), choose] } };
    expect((await submit(editor.agent, clanId, hidden, 1)).status).toBe(403);

    expect((await submit(owner.agent, clanId, choose, 1)).status).toBe(200);
    const tree = await editor.agent.get(`/api/clans/${clanId}/tree`);
    expect(tree.body.progenitorId).toBe(tu.id);
  });
});

describe('GET /api/clans/:id/ops', () => {
  it('lists the ops after `since`, oldest first', async () => {
    const { editor, viewer, clanId } = await setup();
    const first = person();
    const second = person();
    await submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(first), 0);
    await submit(editor.agent, clanId, addPerson(second), 1);

    const all = await viewer.agent.get(`/api/clans/${clanId}/ops`);
    expect(all.status).toBe(200);
    expect(all.body.map((entry: { seq: number }) => entry.seq)).toEqual([1, 2]);
    expect(all.body[0]).toMatchObject({ seq: 1, userId: editor.user.id, op: addPerson(first) });

    const later = await viewer.agent.get(`/api/clans/${clanId}/ops?since=1`);
    expect(later.body).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(later.body[0].op).toEqual(addPerson(second));
  });

  it('validates `since`', async () => {
    const { viewer, clanId } = await setup();
    expect((await viewer.agent.get(`/api/clans/${clanId}/ops?since=-1`)).status).toBe(400);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the routes**

Create `apps/api/src/routes/tree.ts`:
```ts
import {
  IdSchema,
  OPS_PAGE_SIZE,
  OperationListSchema,
  OpsQuerySchema,
  SubmitOpRequestSchema,
  SubmitOpResponseSchema,
  TreeResponseSchema,
  type OperationEntry,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import {
  applyOp,
  needsOwner,
  OpError,
  type ApplyOpResult,
  type Op,
  type TreeState,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import mongoose, { type Types } from 'mongoose';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { AppError } from '../http/AppError.ts';
import type { RouteRegistry } from '../http/routeRegistry.ts';
import { OperationModel } from '../models/Operation.ts';
import { findClan, loadTreeState, saveTreeChanges } from '../tree/store.ts';

const ClanParamsSchema = z.object({ id: IdSchema });
const DUPLICATE_KEY = 11000;

function isDuplicateKey(error: unknown): boolean {
  return (error as { code?: unknown }).code === DUPLICATE_KEY;
}

function applyOrReject(tree: TreeState, op: Op): ApplyOpResult {
  try {
    return applyOp(tree, op);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof OpError) {
      throw new AppError('RULE_VIOLATION', error.message, undefined, error.code);
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

interface OperationRecord {
  seq: number;
  userId: Types.ObjectId;
  type: Op['type'];
  payload: unknown;
  createdAt: Date;
}

function toOperationEntry(record: OperationRecord): OperationEntry {
  return {
    seq: record.seq,
    userId: record.userId.toString(),
    createdAt: record.createdAt.toISOString(),
    // Stored from a parsed op, so type and payload belong together.
    op: { type: record.type, payload: record.payload } as Op,
  };
}

export function registerTreeRoutes(api: RouteRegistry): void {
  api.define({
    method: 'get',
    path: '/clans/:id/tree',
    summary: 'The whole family tree at its current version',
    tags: ['Tree'],
    access: { clanRole: 'viewer' },
    request: { params: ClanParamsSchema },
    responses: { 200: { description: 'People, unions and the version', schema: TreeResponseSchema } },
    handler: async ({ params }) => {
      // One snapshot: the version always matches the people and unions sent with it.
      const body = await mongoose.connection.transaction(
        async (session) => {
          const clan = await findClan(params.id, session);
          const tree = await loadTreeState(clan, session);
          return {
            version: clan.version,
            progenitorId: tree.progenitorId,
            persons: Object.values(tree.persons),
            unions: Object.values(tree.unions),
          };
        },
        { readConcern: { level: 'snapshot' } },
      );
      return { status: 200, body };
    },
  });

  api.define({
    method: 'post',
    path: '/clans/:id/ops',
    summary: 'Apply one change (an op) made against baseVersion',
    tags: ['Tree'],
    access: { clanRole: 'editor' },
    request: { params: ClanParamsSchema, body: SubmitOpRequestSchema },
    responses: {
      200: { description: 'Saved; seq is the new version', schema: SubmitOpResponseSchema },
    },
    handler: async ({ params, body, membership, userId }) => {
      const { op, baseVersion } = body;
      if (needsOwner(op) && membership().role !== 'owner') {
        throw new AppError('FORBIDDEN', 'Only the Manager can choose the progenitor');
      }
      const seq = await mongoose.connection.transaction(async (session) => {
        const clan = await findClan(params.id, session);
        if (clan.version !== baseVersion) {
          throw new AppError(
            'VERSION_CONFLICT',
            `The family tree is at version ${clan.version}, not ${baseVersion}`,
          );
        }
        const before = await loadTreeState(clan, session);
        const { tree: after, inverse } = applyOrReject(before, op);
        try {
          await saveTreeChanges(params.id, before, after, session);
        } catch (error) {
          // The filter includes clanId, so a duplicate _id belongs to another family tree.
          if (!isDuplicateKey(error)) throw error;
          throw new AppError('RULE_VIOLATION', 'This id is already used', undefined, 'DUPLICATE_ID');
        }
        const next = clan.version + 1;
        try {
          await OperationModel.create(
            [{ clanId: clan._id, seq: next, userId: userId(), type: op.type, payload: op.payload, inverse }],
            { session },
          );
        } catch (error) {
          if (!isDuplicateKey(error)) throw error;
          throw new AppError('VERSION_CONFLICT', 'Another change was saved first');
        }
        clan.set({ version: next, progenitorId: after.progenitorId });
        await clan.save({ session });
        return next;
      });
      return { status: 200, body: { seq } };
    },
  });

  api.define({
    method: 'get',
    path: '/clans/:id/ops',
    summary: `Operations after a version, oldest first (at most ${OPS_PAGE_SIZE})`,
    tags: ['Tree'],
    access: { clanRole: 'viewer' },
    request: { params: ClanParamsSchema, query: OpsQuerySchema },
    responses: { 200: { description: 'The operations', schema: OperationListSchema } },
    handler: async ({ params, query }) => {
      const records = await OperationModel.find({ clanId: params.id, seq: { $gt: query.since } })
        .sort({ seq: 1 })
        .limit(OPS_PAGE_SIZE)
        .lean<OperationRecord[]>();
      return { status: 200, body: records.map(toOperationEntry) };
    },
  });
}
```

```ts
describe('DELETE /api/clans/:id', () => {
  /** A family tree with one person, one union, one op, an invite and a removed member. */
  async function filledClan(agent: Parameters<typeof createClan>[0], name: string) {
    const clan = await createClan(agent, { name });
    const personId = newObjectId();
    const op = {
      type: 'batch',
      payload: {
        ops: [
          {
            type: 'addPerson',
            payload: {
              id: personId,
              fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư',
              aliases: [],
              gender: 'male',
              death: { isDeceased: false },
              isAdopted: false,
            },
          },
          {
            type: 'addUnion',
            payload: { id: newObjectId(), partnerIds: [personId], order: 1, status: 'married' },
          },
        ],
      },
    };
    expect((await agent.post(`/api/clans/${clan.id}/ops`).send({ op, baseVersion: 0 })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await agent.post(`/api/clans/${clan.id}/invites`).send({ role: 'viewer' })).status).toBe(201);
    await ClanRemoval.create({ clanId: clan.id, userId: newObjectId(), removedAt: new Date() });
    return clan;
  }

  async function countFor(clanId: string): Promise<number[]> {
    return [
      await Clan.countDocuments({ _id: clanId }),
      await PersonModel.countDocuments({ clanId }),
      await UnionModel.countDocuments({ clanId }),
      await OperationModel.countDocuments({ clanId }),
      await Invite.countDocuments({ clanId }),
      await ClanRemoval.countDocuments({ clanId }),
      await Membership.countDocuments({ clanId }),
    ];
  }

  it('lets the owner delete the family tree and everything in it', async () => {
    const owner = await signUp(app);
    const editor = await signUp(app);
    const doomed = await filledClan(owner.agent, 'Họ Nguyễn');
    const kept = await filledClan(owner.agent, 'Họ Trần');
    await addMember(doomed.id, editor.user.id, 'editor');

    const response = await owner.agent.delete(`/api/clans/${doomed.id}`);
    expect(response.status).toBe(204);
    expect(await countFor(doomed.id)).toEqual([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
    expect(await countFor(kept.id)).toEqual([1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1]);
    expect((await editor.agent.get('/api/clans')).body).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('refuses editors and hides the family tree from strangers', async () => {
    const owner = await signUp(app);
    const editor = await signUp(app);
    const stranger = await signUp(app);
    const clan = await createClan(owner.agent);
    await addMember(clan.id, editor.user.id, 'editor');

    expect((await editor.agent.delete(`/api/clans/${clan.id}`)).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await stranger.agent.delete(`/api/clans/${clan.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect(await Clan.countDocuments({ _id: clan.id })).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `apps/api/src/clans/scoped.ts`:
```ts
import mongoose, { type ClientSession } from 'mongoose';

/**
 * Every model with a clanId. Deleting a clan deletes their documents too; scoped.test.ts fails
 * when a new clan-scoped model is missing here.
 */
export const CLAN_SCOPED_MODEL_NAMES = [
  'Person',
  'Union',
  'Operation',
  'Invite',
  'ClanRemoval',
  'Membership',
] as const;

export async function deleteClanData(clanId: string, session: ClientSession): Promise<void> {
  // One after another: a session runs one operation at a time.
  for (const name of CLAN_SCOPED_MODEL_NAMES) {
    await mongoose.model(name).deleteMany({ clanId }, { session });
  }
}
```

In `apps/api/src/routes/clans.ts`, add the import:
```ts
import { deleteClanData } from '../clans/scoped.ts';
```
and this route at the end of `registerClanRoutes`:
```ts
  api.define({
    method: 'delete',
    path: '/clans/:id',
    summary: 'Delete the clan with its people, history, members and invites',
    tags: ['Clans'],
    access: { clanRole: 'owner' },
    request: { params: ClanParamsSchema },
    responses: { 204: { description: 'Deleted' } },
    handler: async ({ params }) => {
      await mongoose.connection.transaction(async (session) => {
        await deleteClanData(params.id, session);
        await Clan.deleteOne({ _id: params.id }, { session });
      });
      return { status: 204 };
    },
  });
```

### Task 8: Web tree store, API client and op builders

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/web/package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml` (via `pnpm add`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/api.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/ops.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/fixtures.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/ops.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TreeResponseSchema`, `SubmitOpResponseSchema`, `TreeResponse`, `SubmitOpRequest` (`@ancestors-tree/shared`, Task 6); `applyOp`, `OpError`, `EMPTY_TREE`, `treeStateFromLists`, `newObjectId`, `buildTreeIndex`, types `Op`, `TreeState`, `Person`, `Union`, `Gender`, `OpErrorCode`, `TreeIndex` (`@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`); `apiRequest`, `ApiError` (with `reason`, Task 4); `mockApi`, `MockRoutes`.
- Produces:
  - `fetchTree(clanId, signal?): Promise<TreeResponse>`, `submitOp(clanId, request: SubmitOpRequest): Promise<{ seq: number }>`.
  - `useTreeStore` (Zustand) with state `{ clanId: string | null; status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'; loadError: unknown; tree: TreeState; confirmedTree: TreeState; confirmedVersion: number; pending: Op[]; retrying: boolean; notice: TreeNotice | null }` and actions `load(clanId): Promise<void>`, `apply(op): ApplyResult`, `dismissNotice(): void`.
  - `type TreeNotice = { kind: 'reloaded' } | { kind: 'rejected'; error: unknown }`; `type ApplyResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: OpErrorCode }`; `RETRY_DELAYS_MS`; `resetTreeStore()` (tests).
  - Op builders: `interface NewPersonInput { fullName: string; gender: Gender; isDeceased: boolean }`, `buildPerson(input, extra?)`, `addFirstPersonOp(input)`, `addChildOp(index, parentId, input, unionId?)`, `addSpouseOp(index, personId, input)`, `deletePersonOp(id)`, `setProgenitorOp(id)`.
  - Test fixtures (`fixtures.ts`): `CLAN_ID`, `ME`, `CLAN`, `GRANDPA`, `GRANDMA`, `SON`, `LOOSE`, `PARENTS_UNION`, `fixturePerson(id, fullName, overrides?)`, `FAMILY: TreeResponse`, `EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE`, `treeRoutes(tree?, clan?)`.

How the store works (spec §6.2, P5 version of §6.3):
- `tree` is the optimistic tree (`confirmedTree` plus every pending op); `confirmedTree`/`confirmedVersion` are what the server accepted.
- `apply(op)` runs `applyOp` on `tree` at once. A local rule error returns `{ ok: false, reason }` and nothing is queued or sent.
- Pending ops are sent one at a time with `baseVersion = confirmedVersion`. On success the op is applied to `confirmedTree` and `confirmedVersion` becomes `seq`.
- `409 VERSION_CONFLICT` → reload the whole tree, drop pending ops, `notice = { kind: 'reloaded' }`.
- Any other 4xx except 429 (for example `RULE_VIOLATION`, `FORBIDDEN`) → drop that op, rebuild `tree` from `confirmedTree` by replaying the remaining ops (an op that now fails, because it depended on the rejected one, is dropped too), `notice = { kind: 'rejected', error }`.
- Network errors, 429 and 5xx → keep the op, `retrying = true`, retry after 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, then every 30 seconds.
- `load(clanId)` starts a new session: answers that arrive for an older session are ignored. Re-opening the same family tree while ops are still pending keeps the store as it is.

```ts
import type { Clan, TreeResponse } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import type { Person } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import type { MockRoutes } from '../../test/api';

export const CLAN_ID = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60001';
export const ME = {
  id: '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718',
  email: 'lan@example.test',
  name: 'Lan',
  locale: 'vi',
  hasPassword: true,
  hasGoogle: false,
};
export const CLAN: Clan = {
  id: CLAN_ID,
  name: 'Họ Nguyễn',
  hometown: 'Nam Định',
  description: '',
  role: 'owner',
  memberCount: 2,
  createdAt: '2026-11-13T08:00:00.000Z',
};

export const GRANDPA = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a001';
export const GRANDMA = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a002';
export const SON = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a003';
export const LOOSE = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a009';
export const PARENTS_UNION = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6b001';

export function fixturePerson(id: string, fullName: string, overrides: Partial<Person> = {}): Person {
  return {
    id,
    fullName,
    aliases: [],
    gender: 'male',
    death: { isDeceased: false },
    isAdopted: false,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

/** Grandpa Tư (progenitor) and grandma Hoa with their son Nam; Bình is not linked yet. */
export const FAMILY: TreeResponse = {
  version: 3,
  progenitorId: GRANDPA,
  persons: [
    fixturePerson(GRANDPA, 'Nguyễn Văn Tư', {
      birth: { source: 'approx', approxYear: 1931 },
      death: { isDeceased: true, date: { source: 'approx', approxYear: 2004 } },
    }),
    fixturePerson(GRANDMA, 'Lê Thị Hoa', { gender: 'female' }),
    fixturePerson(SON, 'Nguyễn Văn Nam', { parentUnionId: PARENTS_UNION }),
    fixturePerson(LOOSE, 'Trần Văn Bình'),
  ],
  unions: [{ id: PARENTS_UNION, partnerIds: [GRANDPA, GRANDMA], order: 1, status: 'married' }],
};

export const EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE: TreeResponse = {
  version: 0,
  progenitorId: null,
  persons: [],
  unions: [],
};

/** Routes for a signed-in owner looking at one family tree. */
export function treeRoutes(tree: TreeResponse = FAMILY, clan: Clan = CLAN): MockRoutes {
  return {
    'GET /me': () => ({ body: ME }),
    [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}`]: () => ({ body: clan }),
    [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`]: () => ({ body: tree }),
  };
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.test.ts`:
```ts
import type { Op } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { apiErrorReply, mockApi } from '../../test/api';
import {
  CLAN_ID,
  EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE,
  FAMILY,
  fixturePerson,
  GRANDPA,
  LOOSE,
  SON,
  treeRoutes,
} from './fixtures';
import { resetTreeStore, useTreeStore } from './store';

const OPS = `POST /clans/${CLAN_ID}/ops`;
const NEW_ID = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a0f1';
const OTHER_ID = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a0f2';
const addPerson = (id: string, fullName = 'Nguyễn Văn Út'): Op => ({
  type: 'addPerson',
  payload: fixturePerson(id, fullName),
});

const store = () => useTreeStore.getState();

beforeEach(() => {
  resetTreeStore();
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('loading', () => {
  it('loads the tree and its version', async () => {
    mockApi(treeRoutes());
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    expect(store().status).toBe('ready');
    expect(store().confirmedVersion).toBe(3);
    expect(store().tree.progenitorId).toBe(GRANDPA);
    expect(Object.keys(store().tree.persons)).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(store().confirmedTree).toBe(store().tree);
  });

  it('remembers a failed load', async () => {
    mockApi({ [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`]: () => apiErrorReply('NOT_FOUND', 404) });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    expect(store().status).toBe('error');
    expect(store().loadError).toMatchObject({ code: 'NOT_FOUND' });
  });
});

describe('applying ops', () => {
  it('shows a change at once and confirms it after the server accepts it', async () => {
    const api = mockApi({ ...treeRoutes(EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE), [OPS]: () => ({ body: { seq: 1 } }) });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);

    expect(store().apply(addPerson(NEW_ID))).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(store().tree.persons[NEW_ID]?.fullName).toBe('Nguyễn Văn Út');
    expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(1);

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(0));
    expect(store().confirmedVersion).toBe(1);
    expect(store().confirmedTree.persons[NEW_ID]).toBeDefined();
    expect(api.calls.find((call) => call.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({
      op: addPerson(NEW_ID),
      baseVersion: 0,
    });
  });

  it('refuses a change that breaks a rule without sending it', async () => {
    const api = mockApi(treeRoutes());
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    expect(store().apply({ type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: GRANDPA } })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: 'PROGENITOR_PROTECTED',
    });
    expect(store().pending).toEqual([]);
    expect(api.calls.some((call) => call.method === 'POST')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('sends one op at a time, each against the version the last one created', async () => {
    let release: () => void = () => undefined;
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      release = resolve;
    });
    const bodies: unknown[] = [];
    mockApi({
      ...treeRoutes(EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE),
      [OPS]: async ({ body }) => {
        bodies.push(body);
        if (bodies.length === 1) await gate;
        return { body: { seq: bodies.length } };
      },
    });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    store().apply(addPerson(NEW_ID));
    store().apply(addPerson(OTHER_ID, 'Nguyễn Văn Sáu'));

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(bodies).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(2);
    release();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(0));
    expect(bodies).toEqual([
      { op: addPerson(NEW_ID), baseVersion: 0 },
      { op: addPerson(OTHER_ID, 'Nguyễn Văn Sáu'), baseVersion: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(store().confirmedVersion).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe('when the server says no', () => {
  it('reloads the whole tree after a version conflict', async () => {
    let loads = 0;
    mockApi({
      ...treeRoutes(),
      [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`]: () => {
        loads += 1;
        return { body: loads === 1 ? FAMILY : { ...FAMILY, version: 7 } };
      },
      [OPS]: () => apiErrorReply('VERSION_CONFLICT', 409),
    });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    store().apply(addPerson(NEW_ID));

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().notice).toEqual({ kind: 'reloaded' }));
    expect(loads).toBe(2);
    expect(store().confirmedVersion).toBe(7);
    expect(store().pending).toEqual([]);
    expect(store().tree.persons[NEW_ID]).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('rolls back a rejected op, keeps the independent ones and drops the dependent ones', async () => {
    const posted: unknown[] = [];
    mockApi({
      ...treeRoutes(),
      [OPS]: ({ body }) => {
        posted.push(body);
        return posted.length === 1
          ? {
              status: 422,
              body: { error: { code: 'RULE_VIOLATION', message: 'No', reason: 'CYCLE' } },
            }
          : { body: { seq: 4 } };
      },
    });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    // The server will refuse the first op. The second depends on it; the third does not.
    store().apply(addPerson(NEW_ID));
    store().apply({ type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: NEW_ID } });
    store().apply({ type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: LOOSE, changes: { bio: 'Chú họ' } } });

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(0));
    expect(store().notice).toMatchObject({ kind: 'rejected', error: { reason: 'CYCLE' } });
    expect(store().tree.persons[NEW_ID]).toBeUndefined();
    expect(store().tree.progenitorId).toBe(GRANDPA);
    expect(store().tree.persons[LOOSE]?.bio).toBe('Chú họ');
    expect(posted).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(store().confirmedVersion).toBe(4);
  });

  it('keeps an op and retries it after a pause when the server cannot be reached', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] });
    let attempts = 0;
    mockApi({
      ...treeRoutes(),
      [OPS]: () => {
        attempts += 1;
        return attempts === 1 ? { status: 503, body: {} } : { body: { seq: 4 } };
      },
    });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    store().apply({ type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: 'Con trưởng' } } });

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().retrying).toBe(true));
    expect(store().tree.persons[SON]?.bio).toBe('Con trưởng');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(0));
    expect(attempts).toBe(2);
    expect(store().retrying).toBe(false);
  });

  it('forgets the notice when asked', async () => {
    mockApi({ ...treeRoutes(), [OPS]: () => apiErrorReply('FORBIDDEN', 403) });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    store().apply(addPerson(NEW_ID));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().notice?.kind).toBe('rejected'));
    store().dismissNotice();
    expect(store().notice).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('opening the tree again', () => {
  it('keeps unsent changes of the same family tree', async () => {
    mockApi({ ...treeRoutes(), [OPS]: () => new Promise(() => undefined) });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    store().apply(addPerson(NEW_ID));
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(store().tree.persons[NEW_ID]).toBeDefined();
  });
});
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/ops.test.ts`:
```ts
import { applyOp, buildTreeIndex, treeStateFromLists } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { FAMILY, GRANDMA, GRANDPA, LOOSE, PARENTS_UNION, SON } from './fixtures';
import {
  addChildOp,
  addFirstPersonOp,
  addSpouseOp,
  deletePersonOp,
  setProgenitorOp,
  type NewPersonInput,
} from './ops';

const tree = treeStateFromLists(FAMILY.progenitorId, FAMILY.persons, FAMILY.unions);
const index = buildTreeIndex(tree);
const input: NewPersonInput = { fullName: '  Nguyễn Văn Út ', gender: 'male', isDeceased: false };

describe('op builders', () => {
  it('adds a first person with a new id and a trimmed name', () => {
    const op = addFirstPersonOp(input);
    expect(op).toMatchObject({
      type: 'addPerson',
      payload: { fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Út', gender: 'male', death: { isDeceased: false } },
    });
    expect(op.type === 'addPerson' && op.payload.id).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{24}$/);
  });

  it('adds a child to the parent’s first union, as the next sibling', () => {
    const op = addChildOp(index, GRANDPA, input);
    expect(op).toMatchObject({
      type: 'addPerson',
      payload: { parentUnionId: PARENTS_UNION, siblingOrder: 1 },
    });
    // SON has no siblingOrder; the new child is the first with one.
    expect(applyOp(tree, op).tree.persons[SON]).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('creates a one-parent union for a child of someone without a marriage', () => {
    const op = addChildOp(index, LOOSE, input);
    expect(op.type).toBe('batch');
    const next = applyOp(tree, op).tree;
    const union = Object.values(next.unions).find((candidate) =>
      candidate.partnerIds.includes(LOOSE),
    );
    expect(union?.partnerIds).toEqual([LOOSE]);
    expect(Object.values(next.persons).some((p) => p.parentUnionId === union?.id)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('adds a spouse in a new union, or fills a one-parent union', () => {
    const remarried = applyOp(tree, addSpouseOp(index, GRANDPA, input)).tree;
    const grandpaUnions = Object.values(remarried.unions).filter((u) =>
      u.partnerIds.includes(GRANDPA),
    );
    expect(grandpaUnions.map((u) => u.order).sort()).toEqual([1, 2]);

    const withChild = applyOp(tree, addChildOp(index, LOOSE, input)).tree;
    const married = applyOp(withChild, addSpouseOp(buildTreeIndex(withChild), LOOSE, input)).tree;
    const loneUnion = Object.values(married.unions).filter((u) => u.partnerIds.includes(LOOSE));
    expect(loneUnion).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(loneUnion[0]?.partnerIds).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('deletes and chooses the progenitor', () => {
    expect(deletePersonOp(GRANDMA)).toEqual({ type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: GRANDMA } });
    expect(setProgenitorOp(SON)).toEqual({ type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: SON } });
  });
});
```

```ts
import {
  SubmitOpResponseSchema,
  TreeResponseSchema,
  type SubmitOpRequest,
  type SubmitOpResponse,
  type TreeResponse,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { apiRequest } from '../../shared/lib/apiClient';

export function fetchTree(clanId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<TreeResponse> {
  return apiRequest(`/clans/${clanId}/tree`, TreeResponseSchema, { signal });
}

export function submitOp(clanId: string, request: SubmitOpRequest): Promise<SubmitOpResponse> {
  return apiRequest(`/clans/${clanId}/ops`, SubmitOpResponseSchema, {
    method: 'POST',
    json: request,
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the store**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.ts`:
```ts
import {
  applyOp,
  EMPTY_TREE,
  OpError,
  treeStateFromLists,
  type Op,
  type OpErrorCode,
  type TreeState,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { ApiError } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { fetchTree, submitOp } from './api';

export type TreeStatus = 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error';
export type TreeNotice = { kind: 'reloaded' } | { kind: 'rejected'; error: unknown };
export type ApplyResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: OpErrorCode };

/** Waits between retries of an op the server could not take (network, 429, 5xx). */
export const RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [1000, 2000, 4000, 8000, 16000, 30000] as const;

interface TreeStoreState {
  clanId: string | null;
  status: TreeStatus;
  loadError: unknown;
  /** What the user sees: the confirmed tree plus every pending op. */
  tree: TreeState;
  /** What the server has accepted, at confirmedVersion. */
  confirmedTree: TreeState;
  confirmedVersion: number;
  /** Ops not yet accepted, oldest first; pending[0] is the one being sent. */
  pending: Op[];
  /** True while a failed send waits for its retry. */
  retrying: boolean;
  notice: TreeNotice | null;
  load: (clanId: string) => Promise<void>;
  apply: (op: Op) => ApplyResult;
  dismissNotice: () => void;
}

type TreeData = Omit<TreeStoreState, 'load' | 'apply' | 'dismissNotice'>;

const INITIAL: TreeData = {
  clanId: null,
  status: 'idle',
  loadError: null,
  tree: EMPTY_TREE,
  confirmedTree: EMPTY_TREE,
  confirmedVersion: 0,
  pending: [],
  retrying: false,
  notice: null,
};

/** A new session per load(): answers that belong to an older one are ignored. */
let session = 0;
let sending = false;
let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
let attempt = 0;

function clearRetry(): void {
  if (retryTimer !== undefined) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
  retryTimer = undefined;
  attempt = 0;
}

/** Applies ops in order, dropping any that no longer fit (they depended on a rejected op). */
function replay(start: TreeState, ops: readonly Op[]): { tree: TreeState; kept: Op[] } {
  let tree = start;
  const kept: Op[] = [];
  for (const op of ops) {
    try {
      tree = applyOp(tree, op).tree;
      kept.push(op);
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof OpError)) throw error;
    }
  }
  return { tree, kept };
}

export const useTreeStore = create<TreeStoreState>()((set, get) => {
  async function reload(mine: number): Promise<void> {
    const { clanId } = get();
    if (clanId === null) return;
    try {
      const response = await fetchTree(clanId);
      if (mine !== session) return;
      const tree = treeStateFromLists(response.progenitorId, response.persons, response.unions);
      set({
        tree,
        confirmedTree: tree,
        confirmedVersion: response.version,
        pending: [],
        retrying: false,
        notice: { kind: 'reloaded' },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mine === session) set({ status: 'error', loadError: error });
    }
  }

  function reject(error: unknown): void {
    const { confirmedTree, pending } = get();
    const { tree, kept } = replay(confirmedTree, pending.slice(1));
    set({ tree, pending: kept, retrying: false, notice: { kind: 'rejected', error } });
  }

  function scheduleRetry(mine: number): void {
    const delay = RETRY_DELAYS_MS[Math.min(attempt, RETRY_DELAYS_MS.length - 1)];
    attempt += 1;
    set({ retrying: true });
    retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      retryTimer = undefined;
      if (mine === session) void flush();
    }, delay);
  }

  async function recover(error: unknown, mine: number): Promise<void> {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.code === 'VERSION_CONFLICT') {
      attempt = 0;
      await reload(mine);
      return;
    }
    const retryable =
      error instanceof ApiError &&
      (error.status === 0 || error.status === 429 || error.status >= 500);
    if (retryable) {
      scheduleRetry(mine);
      return;
    }
    if (error instanceof ApiError) {
      attempt = 0;
      reject(error);
      return;
    }
    // Not an answer from the server (a bug or a corrupt response): start again from the server.
    await reload(mine);
  }

  async function flush(): Promise<void> {
    if (sending || retryTimer !== undefined) return;
    const { clanId, pending, confirmedVersion } = get();
    const op = pending[0];
    if (clanId === null || op === undefined) return;
    const mine = session;
    sending = true;
    try {
      const { seq } = await submitOp(clanId, { op, baseVersion: confirmedVersion });
      if (mine !== session) return;
      attempt = 0;
      const state = get();
      set({
        confirmedTree: applyOp(state.confirmedTree, op).tree,
        confirmedVersion: seq,
        pending: state.pending.slice(1),
        retrying: false,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mine !== session) return;
      await recover(error, mine);
    } finally {
      if (mine === session) sending = false;
    }
    if (mine === session) void flush();
  }

  return {
    ...INITIAL,

    async load(clanId) {
      const current = get();
      // Coming back to the page while changes are still being saved: keep them.
      if (current.clanId === clanId && current.status === 'ready' && current.pending.length > 0) {
        return;
      }
      session += 1;
      const mine = session;
      sending = false;
      clearRetry();
      set({ ...INITIAL, clanId, status: 'loading' });
      try {
        const response = await fetchTree(clanId);
        if (mine !== session) return;
        const tree = treeStateFromLists(response.progenitorId, response.persons, response.unions);
        set({
          status: 'ready',
          tree,
          confirmedTree: tree,
          confirmedVersion: response.version,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        if (mine === session) set({ status: 'error', loadError: error });
      }
    },

    apply(op) {
      const state = get();
      if (state.status !== 'ready') throw new Error('The tree is not loaded yet');
      try {
        const { tree } = applyOp(state.tree, op);
        set({ tree, pending: [...state.pending, op] });
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof OpError) return { ok: false, reason: error.code };
        throw error;
      }
      void flush();
      return { ok: true };
    },

    dismissNotice() {
      set({ notice: null });
    },
  };
});

/** Tests: forget everything, including the module-level send state. */
export function resetTreeStore(): void {
  session += 1;
  sending = false;
  clearRetry();
  useTreeStore.setState(INITIAL);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the op builders**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/ops.ts`:
```ts
import {
  newObjectId,
  type Gender,
  type Op,
  type Person,
  type SingleOp,
  type TreeIndex,
  type Union,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';

/** What the P5 form asks (dates arrive with DualDateInput in P6). */
export interface NewPersonInput {
  fullName: string;
  gender: Gender;
  isDeceased: boolean;
}

const MAX_ORDER = 99;

export function buildPerson(input: NewPersonInput, extra: Partial<Person> = {}): Person {
  return {
    id: newObjectId(),
    fullName: input.fullName.trim(),
    aliases: [],
    gender: input.gender,
    death: { isDeceased: input.isDeceased },
    isAdopted: false,
    ...extra,
  };
}

export function addFirstPersonOp(input: NewPersonInput): Op {
  return { type: 'addPerson', payload: buildPerson(input) };
}

/**
 * A child of `parentId` in `unionId` (default: the parent's first union). Someone without a
 * union gets a one-parent union first, in the same batch.
 */
export function addChildOp(
  index: TreeIndex,
  parentId: string,
  input: NewPersonInput,
  unionId?: string,
): Op {
  const target = unionId ?? index.unionsOf(parentId)[0]?.id;
  if (target === undefined) {
    const union: Union = { id: newObjectId(), partnerIds: [parentId], order: 1, status: 'married' };
    return {
      type: 'batch',
      payload: {
        ops: [
          { type: 'addUnion', payload: union },
          {
            type: 'addPerson',
            payload: buildPerson(input, { parentUnionId: union.id, siblingOrder: 1 }),
          },
        ],
      },
    };
  }
  const last = Math.max(0, ...index.childrenOf(target).map((child) => child.siblingOrder ?? 0));
  const extra: Partial<Person> = { parentUnionId: target };
  if (last < MAX_ORDER) extra.siblingOrder = last + 1;
  return { type: 'addPerson', payload: buildPerson(input, extra) };
}

/**
 * A spouse of `personId`. When the person has a one-parent union (children added before the
 * spouse), the spouse joins it; otherwise a new union comes after the existing ones.
 */
export function addSpouseOp(index: TreeIndex, personId: string, input: NewPersonInput): Op {
  const spouse = buildPerson(input);
  const unions = index.unionsOf(personId);
  const alone = unions.find((union) => union.partnerIds.length === 1);
  const link: SingleOp =
    alone !== undefined
      ? {
          type: 'updateUnion',
          payload: { id: alone.id, changes: { partnerIds: [personId, spouse.id] } },
        }
      : {
          type: 'addUnion',
          payload: {
            id: newObjectId(),
            partnerIds: [personId, spouse.id],
            order: Math.min(MAX_ORDER, Math.max(0, ...unions.map((union) => union.order)) + 1),
            status: 'married',
          },
        };
  return { type: 'batch', payload: { ops: [{ type: 'addPerson', payload: spouse }, link] } };
}

export function deletePersonOp(id: string): Op {
  return { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id } };
}

export function setProgenitorOp(personId: string | null): Op {
  return { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId } };
}
```

```
feat(web): tree store with optimistic ops

Adds @xyflow/react and zustand. useTreeStore applies each op locally
with applyOp, queues it and sends one op at a time against the last
confirmed version. A 409 reloads the whole tree, a rejected op is
rolled back (later ops are replayed on the confirmed tree), and
network or server errors are retried with backoff. Op builders cover
the P5 edits: first person, child, spouse, delete, progenitor.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
```

### Task 9: Pure layout and React Flow elements

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/layout.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/flowElements.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/features/tree/layout.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/flowElements.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `buildTreeIndex`, `classifyLineage`, `computeGenerations`, `treeStateFromLists`, `TreeState`, `Person`, `Union` (`@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`); `POLAROID_SIZE`, `pinPoint`, `THREAD_DROP`, `Point`, `TapeKind` (`../../shared/ui`); `Node`, `Edge` types (`@xyflow/react`).
- Produces:
  - `layout.ts`: constants `CARD` (`POLAROID_SIZE.full`, 100 × 138), `SPOUSE_GAP = 48`, `SIBLING_GAP = 40`, `ROW_GAP = 96`, `ROW_HEIGHT = CARD.height + ROW_GAP`, `KNOT_SIZE = 12`; types `PlacedPerson { id; x; y; tape: TapeKind; generation: number }`, `PlacedUnion { id; x; y; width; height; couple: boolean }`, `PlacedThread { childId; unionId; from: Point; dropTo: number; to: Point }`, `TreeLayout { persons; unions; threads; unlinked: string[]; width; height }`; `layoutTree(tree: TreeState): TreeLayout`.
  - `flowElements.ts`: `PersonNodeData = { personId: string; tape: TapeKind; generation: number }`, `UnionNodeData = { couple: boolean }`, `ThreadEdgeData = { from: Point; dropTo: number; to: Point }`, `PersonFlowNode = Node<PersonNodeData, 'person'>`, `UnionFlowNode = Node<UnionNodeData, 'union'>`, `TreeFlowNode`, `ThreadFlowEdge = Edge<ThreadEdgeData, 'thread'>`, `FlowElements { nodes: TreeFlowNode[]; edges: ThreadFlowEdge[] }`, `unionNodeId(unionId)`, `toFlowElements(layout, previous?): FlowElements`.

The layout follows spec §7.4 steps 1–4 on the main thread (the Web Worker, collapsing and level of detail are P7):
1. `classifyLineage` and `computeGenerations`.
2. Family units, depth-first from the progenitor in display order: a blood member, the in-law spouses drawn beside them (first spouse on the right, second on the left, and so on), and the children of each of their unions. Each union is drawn once, under the first blood partner reached. A partner who is also blood (two relatives married) or an in-law already drawn beside someone else is not drawn again: that union hangs from a knot under the card.
3. Widths bottom-up: a unit is as wide as its row of cards or its children's subtrees side by side, whichever is wider.
4. Positions top-down: the row is centred in its unit; the children are centred below in the order of their union's knots, so threads do not cross. Every subtree owns its own horizontal band, so cards never overlap.

Tapes (spec §13.3): the progenitor wears `'progenitor'` (vermilion), in-laws `'in-law'` (grey), blood relatives the number of the progenitor's child they descend from (`0, 1, 2…`, cycling through the branch palette). People not linked to the progenitor are not drawn; `unlinked` lists them for the "Not yet linked" tray (their tape there is `'loose'`).

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/layout.test.ts`:
```ts
import {
  treeStateFromLists,
  type Person,
  type TreeState,
  type Union,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { pinPoint } from '../../shared/ui';
import { CARD, layoutTree, ROW_HEIGHT, SPOUSE_GAP, type TreeLayout } from './layout';

const id = (n: number) => n.toString(16).padStart(24, '0');
const A = id(1);
const B = id(2);
const C = id(3);
const D = id(4);
const E = id(5);
const F = id(6);
const G = id(7);
const X = id(9);
const U1 = id(101);
const U2 = id(102);
const U3 = id(103);

function person(personId: string, overrides: Partial<Person> = {}): Person {
  return {
    id: personId,
    fullName: `Person ${personId.slice(-2)}`,
    aliases: [],
    gender: 'male',
    death: { isDeceased: false },
    isAdopted: false,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function union(unionId: string, partnerIds: string[], order = 1): Union {
  return { id: unionId, partnerIds, order, status: 'married' };
}

function placed(layout: TreeLayout, personId: string) {
  const found = layout.persons.find((candidate) => candidate.id === personId);
  if (!found) throw new Error(`${personId} was not placed`);
  return found;
}

function overlaps(layout: TreeLayout): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  const cards = layout.persons;
  for (let i = 0; i < cards.length; i += 1) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < cards.length; j += 1) {
      const a = cards[i] as (typeof cards)[number];
      const b = cards[j] as (typeof cards)[number];
      const apart =
        a.x + CARD.width <= b.x ||
        b.x + CARD.width <= a.x ||
        a.y + CARD.height <= b.y ||
        b.y + CARD.height <= a.y;
      if (!apart) problems.push(`${a.id} overlaps ${b.id}`);
    }
  }
  return problems;
}

/** A + B with children C and D. */
const couple = treeStateFromLists(
  A,
  [person(A), person(B, { gender: 'female' }), person(C, { parentUnionId: U1 }), person(D, { parentUnionId: U1 })],
  [union(U1, [A, B])],
);

describe('layoutTree', () => {
  it('draws nothing without a progenitor and lists everyone as not linked', () => {
    const layout = layoutTree({ ...couple, progenitorId: null });
    expect(layout.persons).toEqual([]);
    expect(layout.unlinked).toEqual([A, B, C, D]);
  });

  it('places a lone progenitor at the origin with the progenitor tape', () => {
    const layout = layoutTree(treeStateFromLists(A, [person(A), person(X)], []));
    expect(layout.persons).toEqual([{ id: A, x: 0, y: 0, tape: 'progenitor', generation: 1 }]);
    expect(layout.unlinked).toEqual([X]);
    expect(layout).toMatchObject({ width: CARD.width, height: CARD.height });
  });

  it('puts a wife beside her husband and their children centred below the stitch', () => {
    const layout = layoutTree(couple);
    const husband = placed(layout, A);
    const wife = placed(layout, B);
    expect(wife.x).toBe(husband.x + CARD.width + SPOUSE_GAP);
    expect(wife.tape).toBe('in-law');
    expect(layout.unions).toEqual([
      { id: U1, x: husband.x + CARD.width, y: 0, width: SPOUSE_GAP, height: CARD.height, couple: true },
    ]);

    const [son, daughter] = [placed(layout, C), placed(layout, D)];
    expect(son.y).toBe(ROW_HEIGHT);
    expect(son.generation).toBe(2);
    expect([son.tape, daughter.tape]).toEqual([0, 1]);
    const knotX = husband.x + CARD.width + SPOUSE_GAP / 2;
    expect((son.x + daughter.x + CARD.width) / 2).toBe(knotX);

    const thread = layout.threads.find((candidate) => candidate.childId === C);
    expect(thread).toEqual({
      childId: C,
      unionId: U1,
      from: { x: knotX, y: CARD.height / 2 },
      dropTo: CARD.height + 8,
      to: pinPoint(son, CARD),
    });
  });

  it('puts a second wife on the left', () => {
    const tree = treeStateFromLists(
      A,
      [person(A), person(B, { gender: 'female' }), person(E, { gender: 'female' })],
      [union(U1, [A, B], 1), union(U2, [A, E], 2)],
    );
    const layout = layoutTree(tree);
    expect(placed(layout, E).x).toBeLessThan(placed(layout, A).x);
    expect(placed(layout, A).x).toBeLessThan(placed(layout, B).x);
  });

  it('hangs the children of a parent without a spouse from a knot under the card', () => {
    const tree = treeStateFromLists(A, [person(A), person(C, { parentUnionId: U1 })], [union(U1, [A])]);
    const layout = layoutTree(tree);
    expect(layout.unions).toEqual([
      { id: U1, x: CARD.width / 2 - 6, y: CARD.height - 6, width: 12, height: 12, couple: false },
    ]);
    expect(layout.threads[0]?.from).toEqual({ x: CARD.width / 2, y: CARD.height });
  });

  it('draws a marriage between two relatives once, and each person once', () => {
    // A → C and D (U1, A alone); C → F (U2); D marries F (U3) and they have G.
    const tree = treeStateFromLists(
      A,
      [
        person(A),
        person(C, { parentUnionId: U1 }),
        person(D, { parentUnionId: U1, gender: 'female' }),
        person(F, { parentUnionId: U2 }),
        person(G, { parentUnionId: U3 }),
      ],
      [union(U1, [A]), union(U2, [C]), union(U3, [D, F])],
    );
    const layout = layoutTree(tree);
    expect(layout.persons.map((p) => p.id).sort()).toEqual([A, C, D, F, G].sort());
    expect(layout.unions.filter((u) => u.id === U3)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(layout.threads.filter((t) => t.childId === G)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(overlaps(layout)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('never overlaps cards and always hangs children below their parents', () => {
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 25; seed += 1) {
      const tree = randomFamily(seed, 120);
      const layout = layoutTree(tree);
      expect(layout.unlinked, `seed ${seed}`).toEqual([]);
      expect(layout.persons, `seed ${seed}`).toHaveLength(Object.keys(tree.persons).length);
      expect(overlaps(layout), `seed ${seed}`).toEqual([]);
      for (const thread of layout.threads) {
        expect(thread.to.y, `seed ${seed}`).toBeGreaterThan(thread.dropTo);
        expect(thread.dropTo, `seed ${seed}`).toBeGreaterThan(thread.from.y);
      }
    }
  });
});

/** A random descendant tree with spouses and second marriages; deterministic per seed. */
function randomFamily(seed: number, size: number): TreeState {
  let state = seed;
  const random = () => {
    state = (Math.imul(state, 1664525) + 1013904223) >>> 0;
    return state / 2 ** 32;
  };
  let next = 1;
  const newId = () => id((next += 1) * 7);
  const root = newId();
  const persons: Person[] = [person(root)];
  const unions: Union[] = [];
  const blood = [root];
  while (persons.length < size) {
    const parent = blood[Math.floor(random() * blood.length)] as string;
    const own = unions.filter((u) => u.partnerIds.includes(parent));
    if (random() < 0.3 && own.length < 3) {
      const spouse = newId();
      persons.push(person(spouse, { gender: 'female' }));
      unions.push(union(newId(), [parent, spouse], own.length + 1));
      continue;
    }
    let target = own[Math.floor(random() * own.length)];
    if (!target) {
      target = union(newId(), [parent]);
      unions.push(target);
    }
    const child = newId();
    persons.push(person(child, { parentUnionId: target.id }));
    blood.push(child);
  }
  return treeStateFromLists(root, persons, unions);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the layout**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/layout.ts`:
```ts
import {
  buildTreeIndex,
  classifyLineage,
  computeGenerations,
  type TreeState,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { pinPoint, POLAROID_SIZE, THREAD_DROP, type Point, type TapeKind } from '../../shared/ui';

export const CARD = POLAROID_SIZE.full;
/** Between a person and their spouse; the marriage stitch runs through it. */
export const SPOUSE_GAP = 48;
/** Between neighbouring subtrees. */
export const SIBLING_GAP = 40;
/** Between generations; threads drop and bend here. */
export const ROW_GAP = 96;
export const ROW_HEIGHT = CARD.height + ROW_GAP;
/** The knot under a parent who has children without a spouse on the board. */
export const KNOT_SIZE = 12;

export interface PlacedPerson {
  id: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  tape: TapeKind;
  generation: number;
}

export interface PlacedUnion {
  id: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  /** True: a stitch between two cards. False: a knot under one card. */
  couple: boolean;
}

export interface PlacedThread {
  childId: string;
  unionId: string;
  from: Point;
  dropTo: number;
  to: Point;
}

export interface TreeLayout {
  persons: PlacedPerson[];
  unions: PlacedUnion[];
  threads: PlacedThread[];
  /** Not linked to the progenitor: listed in the tray, not drawn. Sorted by id. */
  unlinked: string[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

interface UnionBlock {
  unionId: string;
  /** The in-law drawn beside the unit's person, or null when the union hangs from a knot. */
  spouseId: string | null;
  children: Unit[];
}

/** A blood member, the spouses drawn beside them and their children's units (spec §7.4). */
interface Unit {
  personId: string;
  /** Index of the progenitor's child this person descends from; null for the progenitor. */
  branch: number | null;
  rights: string[];
  lefts: string[];
  unions: UnionBlock[];
  rowWidth: number;
  width: number;
}

const STEP = CARD.width + SPOUSE_GAP;

function rowWidthOf(cards: number): number {
  return cards * CARD.width + (cards - 1) * SPOUSE_GAP;
}

export function layoutTree(tree: TreeState): TreeLayout {
  const index = buildTreeIndex(tree);
  const lineage = classifyLineage(tree, index);
  const generations = computeGenerations(tree, index);
  const result: TreeLayout = { persons: [], unions: [], threads: [], unlinked: [], width: 0, height: 0 };
  const drawn = new Set<string>();
  const claimed = new Set<string>();
  let nextBranch = 0;

  // Steps 2 and 3: build the units depth-first and size them on the way back up.
  function buildUnit(personId: string, branch: number | null): Unit {
    drawn.add(personId);
    const unit: Unit = { personId, branch, rights: [], lefts: [], unions: [], rowWidth: 0, width: 0 };
    for (const union of index.unionsOf(personId)) {
      if (claimed.has(union.id)) continue;
      claimed.add(union.id);
      const partner = union.partnerIds.find((partnerId) => partnerId !== personId);
      let spouseId: string | null = null;
      if (partner !== undefined && !drawn.has(partner) && lineage.get(partner) !== 'blood') {
        drawn.add(partner);
        (unit.rights.length <= unit.lefts.length ? unit.rights : unit.lefts).push(partner);
        spouseId = partner;
      }
      const children: Unit[] = [];
      for (const child of index.childrenOf(union.id)) {
        if (drawn.has(child.id)) continue;
        children.push(buildUnit(child.id, branch ?? nextBranch++));
      }
      unit.unions.push({ unionId: union.id, spouseId, children });
    }
    unit.rowWidth = rowWidthOf(1 + unit.rights.length + unit.lefts.length);
    const children = unit.unions.flatMap((block) => block.children);
    const childrenWidth =
      children.reduce((sum, child) => sum + child.width, 0) +
      Math.max(0, children.length - 1) * SIBLING_GAP;
    unit.width = Math.max(unit.rowWidth, childrenWidth);
    return unit;
  }

  function tapeOf(personId: string, unit: Unit): TapeKind {
    if (personId === tree.progenitorId) return 'progenitor';
    if (personId !== unit.personId) return 'in-law';
    return unit.branch ?? 0;
  }

  // Step 4: positions top-down. Returns the x of the unit's own card.
  function place(unit: Unit, left: number, top: number): number {
    const rowLeft = left + (unit.width - unit.rowWidth) / 2;
    const row = [...[...unit.lefts].reverse(), unit.personId, ...unit.rights];
    const xOf = new Map<string, number>();
    row.forEach((personId, position) => {
      const x = rowLeft + position * STEP;
      xOf.set(personId, x);
      result.persons.push({
        id: personId,
        x,
        y: top,
        tape: tapeOf(personId, unit),
        generation: generations.get(personId) ?? 0,
      });
    });
    result.height = Math.max(result.height, top + CARD.height);
    const selfX = rowLeft + unit.lefts.length * STEP;

    const knots = unit.unions.map((block) => {
      const spouseX = block.spouseId === null ? undefined : xOf.get(block.spouseId);
      if (spouseX !== undefined) {
        const x = Math.min(selfX, spouseX) + CARD.width;
        const width = Math.max(selfX, spouseX) - x;
        result.unions.push({ id: block.unionId, x, y: top, width, height: CARD.height, couple: true });
        return { block, knot: { x: x + width / 2, y: top + CARD.height / 2 } };
      }
      const knot = { x: selfX + CARD.width / 2, y: top + CARD.height };
      if (block.children.length > 0) {
        result.unions.push({
          id: block.unionId,
          x: knot.x - KNOT_SIZE / 2,
          y: knot.y - KNOT_SIZE / 2,
          width: KNOT_SIZE,
          height: KNOT_SIZE,
          couple: false,
        });
      }
      return { block, knot };
    });

    const withChildren = knots
      .filter(({ block }) => block.children.length > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => a.knot.x - b.knot.x);
    const children = withChildren.flatMap(({ block }) => block.children);
    const total =
      children.reduce((sum, child) => sum + child.width, 0) +
      Math.max(0, children.length - 1) * SIBLING_GAP;
    let cursor = left + (unit.width - total) / 2;
    const childTop = top + ROW_HEIGHT;
    for (const { block, knot } of withChildren) {
      for (const child of block.children) {
        const childX = place(child, cursor, childTop);
        result.threads.push({
          childId: child.personId,
          unionId: block.unionId,
          from: knot,
          dropTo: top + CARD.height + THREAD_DROP,
          to: pinPoint({ x: childX, y: childTop }, CARD),
        });
        cursor += child.width + SIBLING_GAP;
      }
    }
    return selfX;
  }

  const root = tree.progenitorId;
  if (root !== null && tree.persons[root]) {
    const unit = buildUnit(root, null);
    place(unit, 0, 0);
    result.width = unit.width;
  }
  result.unlinked = Object.keys(tree.persons)
    .filter((personId) => !drawn.has(personId))
    .sort();
  return result;
}
```
