- [ ] **Step 6: Write the element builder**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/flowElements.ts`:
```ts
import type { Edge, Node } from '@xyflow/react';
import type { Point, TapeKind } from '../../shared/ui';
import { CARD, type TreeLayout } from './layout';

// React Flow needs `type` aliases (not interfaces) for node and edge data.
export type PersonNodeData = { personId: string; tape: TapeKind; generation: number };
export type UnionNodeData = { couple: boolean };
export type ThreadEdgeData = { from: Point; dropTo: number; to: Point };

export type PersonFlowNode = Node<PersonNodeData, 'person'>;
export type UnionFlowNode = Node<UnionNodeData, 'union'>;
export type TreeFlowNode = PersonFlowNode | UnionFlowNode;
export type ThreadFlowEdge = Edge<ThreadEdgeData, 'thread'>;

export interface FlowElements {
  nodes: TreeFlowNode[];
  edges: ThreadFlowEdge[];
}

export function unionNodeId(unionId: string): string {
  return `union-${unionId}`;
}

function samePoint(a: Point, b: Point): boolean {
  return a.x === b.x && a.y === b.y;
}

function sameNode(a: TreeFlowNode, b: TreeFlowNode): boolean {
  if (a.type !== b.type || !samePoint(a.position, b.position)) return false;
  if (a.width !== b.width || a.height !== b.height) return false;
  const left = a.data as Record<string, unknown>;
  const right = b.data as Record<string, unknown>;
  return Object.keys(left).every((key) => left[key] === right[key]);
}

function sameEdge(a: ThreadFlowEdge, b: ThreadFlowEdge): boolean {
  return (
    a.source === b.source &&
    a.target === b.target &&
    a.data !== undefined &&
    b.data !== undefined &&
    samePoint(a.data.from, b.data.from) &&
    a.data.dropTo === b.data.dropTo &&
    samePoint(a.data.to, b.data.to)
  );
}

/**
 * Nodes and edges for React Flow. Objects that did not change are reused from `previous`, so
 * React Flow and the memoised node components skip them (spec §7.4). Unions come first so the
 * cards are drawn on top of the stitches.
 */
export function toFlowElements(layout: TreeLayout, previous?: FlowElements): FlowElements {
  const oldNodes = new Map(previous?.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node] as const));
  const oldEdges = new Map(previous?.edges.map((edge) => [edge.id, edge] as const));
  const keepNode = (node: TreeFlowNode): TreeFlowNode => {
    const old = oldNodes.get(node.id);
    return old && sameNode(old, node) ? old : node;
  };

  const nodes: TreeFlowNode[] = [
    ...layout.unions.map((union) =>
      keepNode({
        id: unionNodeId(union.id),
        type: 'union',
        position: { x: union.x, y: union.y },
        width: union.width,
        height: union.height,
        data: { couple: union.couple },
      }),
    ),
    ...layout.persons.map((person) =>
      keepNode({
        id: person.id,
        type: 'person',
        position: { x: person.x, y: person.y },
        width: CARD.width,
        height: CARD.height,
        data: { personId: person.id, tape: person.tape, generation: person.generation },
      }),
    ),
  ];

  const edges = layout.threads.map((thread): ThreadFlowEdge => {
    const edge: ThreadFlowEdge = {
      id: `thread-${thread.childId}`,
      type: 'thread',
      source: unionNodeId(thread.unionId),
      target: thread.childId,
      data: { from: thread.from, dropTo: thread.dropTo, to: thread.to },
    };
    const old = oldEdges.get(edge.id);
    return old && sameEdge(old, edge) ? old : edge;
  });

  return { nodes, edges };
}
```

### Task 10: Render the board with React Flow

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/test/reactFlow.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/treeActions.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonCard.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonNode.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/UnionNode.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/ThreadEdge.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeBoard.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/locales/vi.json`, `apps/web/src/locales/en.json`
- Test: `apps/web/src/features/tree/format.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeBoard.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useTreeStore`, `resetTreeStore` (Task 8); `layoutTree` (Task 9); `toFlowElements`, `PersonFlowNode`, `UnionFlowNode`, `ThreadFlowEdge`, `FlowElements` (Task 9); `Polaroid`, `Stitch`, `Thread`, `Button`, `tiltFor`, `useReducedMotion`, `TapeKind` (`../../shared/ui`); fixtures (Task 8).
- Produces:
  - `mockReactFlow()` (`apps/web/src/test/reactFlow.ts`): jsdom shims for `ResizeObserver`, `DOMMatrixReadOnly`, `offsetWidth`/`offsetHeight` and `SVGElement.getBBox`. Call it in `beforeEach` (it uses `vi.stubGlobal`, which `vi.unstubAllGlobals()` undoes).
  - `yearOf(date?)`, `lifeYears(person): string | undefined`, `cardLabel(t, name, years, generation): string` (`format.ts`).
  - `TreeActionsContext: Context<((personId: string) => void) | null>` (`treeActions.ts`): when set, every card shows an actions button that calls it.
  - `PersonCard({ person, tape, generation })`, memoised `PersonNode`, `UnionNode`, `ThreadEdge`, and `TreeBoard({ label })` — a `role="group"` board that draws the progenitor's tree, or a hint when no progenitor is chosen.
  - Locale keys `tree.board.label`, `tree.board.noProgenitor`, `tree.card.label`, `tree.card.labelNoYears`, `tree.card.actions`.

Performance rules this task sets up (spec §7.4): `nodeTypes`/`edgeTypes` are module constants; `PersonNode`, `UnionNode` and `ThreadEdge` are `React.memo`; `PersonNode` reads its person with `useTreeStore((s) => s.tree.persons[id])`, so an edit re-renders one card; `toFlowElements` reuses unchanged node objects. Nodes carry `width`/`height`, so React Flow shows them without measuring first. Date marks (death anniversary, birthday) need lunar dates and arrive with `DualDateInput` in P6; P5 cards show life years.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the React Flow test shims**

Create `apps/web/src/test/reactFlow.ts`:
```ts
import { vi } from 'vitest';

/** jsdom has no layout engine; React Flow observes sizes and reads the viewport transform. */
class ResizeObserverShim {
  private readonly callback: ResizeObserverCallback;

  constructor(callback: ResizeObserverCallback) {
    this.callback = callback;
  }

  observe(target: Element): void {
    this.callback([{ target } as ResizeObserverEntry], this as unknown as ResizeObserver);
  }

  unobserve(): void {
    // Nothing is observed for real.
  }

  disconnect(): void {
    // Nothing is observed for real.
  }
}

class DOMMatrixReadOnlyShim {
  readonly m22: number;

  constructor(transform?: string) {
    const scale = transform?.match(/scale\(([\d.]+)\)/)?.[1];
    this.m22 = scale === undefined ? 1 : Number(scale);
  }
}

let prototypesPatched = false;

/** Call in beforeEach: vi.unstubAllGlobals() in afterEach removes the global shims. */
export function mockReactFlow(): void {
  vi.stubGlobal('ResizeObserver', ResizeObserverShim);
  vi.stubGlobal('DOMMatrixReadOnly', DOMMatrixReadOnlyShim);
  if (prototypesPatched) return;
  prototypesPatched = true;
  Object.defineProperties(HTMLElement.prototype, {
    offsetHeight: {
      configurable: true,
      get(this: HTMLElement) {
        return Number.parseFloat(this.style.height) || 1;
      },
    },
    offsetWidth: {
      configurable: true,
      get(this: HTMLElement) {
        return Number.parseFloat(this.style.width) || 1;
      },
    },
  });
  Object.defineProperty(SVGElement.prototype, 'getBBox', {
    configurable: true,
    value: () => ({ x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0 }),
  });
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

    expect(yearOf({ source: 'solar', solar: { day: 1, month: 2, year: 1931 } })).toBe('1931');
    expect(yearOf({ source: 'lunar', lunar: { day: 15, month: 10, year: 1930, isLeap: false } })).toBe(
      '1930',
    );
    expect(yearOf({ source: 'approx', approxYear: 1900 })).toBe('1900');
    expect(yearOf(undefined)).toBeUndefined();

    expect(lifeYears(fixturePerson(ID, 'A', { birth: born }))).toBe('1931');
    expect(
      lifeYears(fixturePerson(ID, 'A', { birth: born, death: { isDeceased: true, date: died } })),
    ).toBe('1931–2004');
    expect(lifeYears(fixturePerson(ID, 'A', { birth: born, death: { isDeceased: true } }))).toBe(
      '1931–?',
    );
    expect(lifeYears(fixturePerson(ID, 'A', { death: { isDeceased: true, date: died } }))).toBe(
      '?–2004',
    );
```

```tsx
import { treeStateFromLists, type TreeState } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { act } from 'react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { mockApi } from '../../test/api';
import { mockReactFlow } from '../../test/reactFlow';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { CLAN_ID, FAMILY, SON } from './fixtures';
import { resetTreeStore, useTreeStore } from './store';
import { TreeActionsContext } from './treeActions';
import { TreeBoard } from './TreeBoard';

function showTree(tree: TreeState): void {
  useTreeStore.setState({
    clanId: CLAN_ID,
    status: 'ready',
    tree,
    confirmedTree: tree,
    confirmedVersion: 3,
  });
}

const family = treeStateFromLists(FAMILY.progenitorId, FAMILY.persons, FAMILY.unions);

beforeEach(() => {
  mockReactFlow();
  resetTreeStore();
});

afterEach(() => {
  resetTreeStore();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('TreeBoard', () => {
  it('draws everyone linked to the progenitor with name, years, generation and tape', async () => {
    showTree(family);
    renderWithProviders(<TreeBoard label="Cây gia phả: Họ Nguyễn" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Cây gia phả: Họ Nguyễn' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Văn Tư, 1931–2004, đời thứ 1' }),
    ).toHaveAttribute('data-tape', 'progenitor');
    expect(screen.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Lê Thị Hoa, đời thứ 1' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'data-tape',
      'in-law',
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Văn Nam, đời thứ 2' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'data-tape',
      'branch-1',
    );
    // Not linked to the progenitor: listed in the tray, not drawn.
    expect(screen.queryByRole('figure', { name: /Trần Văn Bình/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows an edit at once', async () => {
    mockApi({ [`POST /clans/${CLAN_ID}/ops`]: () => ({ body: { seq: 4 } }) });
    showTree(family);
    renderWithProviders(<TreeBoard label="Cây gia phả" />);
    await screen.findByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Văn Nam, đời thứ 2' });
    act(() => {
      useTreeStore.getState().apply({
        type: 'updatePerson',
        payload: { id: SON, changes: { fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Năm' } },
      });
    });
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Văn Năm, đời thứ 2' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('asks for a progenitor when none is chosen', () => {
    showTree({ ...family, progenitorId: null });
    renderWithProviders(<TreeBoard label="Cây gia phả" />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Chưa chọn thuỷ tổ/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('group')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('offers a card’s actions only when editing is possible', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const openActions = vi.fn();
    showTree(family);
    const { unmount } = renderWithProviders(<TreeBoard label="Cây gia phả" />);
    await screen.findByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Văn Nam, đời thứ 2' });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Thao tác với/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    unmount();

    renderWithProviders(
      <TreeActionsContext value={openActions}>
        <TreeBoard label="Cây gia phả" />
      </TreeActionsContext>,
    );
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Thao tác với Nguyễn Văn Nam' }));
    expect(openActions).toHaveBeenCalledWith(SON);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the formatting helpers and the actions context**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/format.ts`:
```ts
import type { DateInfo, Person } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import type { TFunction } from 'i18next';

/** The year on the side the user entered (approximate years are shown as plain years in P5). */
export function yearOf(date: DateInfo | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (!date) return undefined;
  const year =
    date.source === 'solar'
      ? date.solar?.year
      : date.source === 'lunar'
        ? date.lunar?.year
        : date.approxYear;
  return year === undefined ? undefined : String(year);
}

/** "1931–2004", "1962" for the living, "1931–?" or "?–2004" when one year is unknown. */
export function lifeYears(person: Person): string | undefined {
  const born = yearOf(person.birth);
  const died = person.death.isDeceased ? yearOf(person.death.date) : undefined;
  if (born !== undefined && died !== undefined) return `${born}–${died}`;
  if (born !== undefined) return person.death.isDeceased ? `${born}–?` : born;
  if (died !== undefined) return `?–${died}`;
  return undefined;
}

/** The card's accessible name: name, life years and generation (spec §7.8). */
export function cardLabel(
  t: TFunction,
  name: string,
  years: string | undefined,
  generation: number,
): string {
  return years === undefined
    ? t('tree.card.labelNoYears', { name, generation })
    : t('tree.card.label', { name, years, generation });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the node and edge components**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Person } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { Ellipsis } from 'lucide-react';
import { useContext } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Button, Polaroid, tiltFor, type TapeKind } from '../../shared/ui';
import { cardLabel, lifeYears } from './format';
import { TreeActionsContext } from './treeActions';

interface PersonCardProps {
  person: Person;
  tape: TapeKind;
  generation: number;
}

export function PersonCard({ person, tape, generation }: PersonCardProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const openActions = useContext(TreeActionsContext);
  const years = lifeYears(person);
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <Polaroid
        name={person.fullName}
        label={cardLabel(t, person.fullName, years, generation)}
        lifeYears={years}
        tape={tape}
        tilt={tiltFor(person.id)}
      />
      {openActions ? (
        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          size="icon"
          // nodrag/nopan: React Flow leaves clicks on this button alone.
          className="nodrag nopan absolute -top-3 -right-3 size-8 rounded-full"
          aria-label={t('tree.card.actions', { name: person.fullName })}
          onClick={() => openActions(person.id)}
        >
          <Ellipsis aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonNode.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Handle, Position, type NodeProps } from '@xyflow/react';
import { memo, type CSSProperties } from 'react';
import type { PersonFlowNode } from './flowElements';
import { PersonCard } from './PersonCard';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';

/** Edges need a handle to attach to; nobody connects nodes by hand. */
const HIDDEN_HANDLE: CSSProperties = { opacity: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' };

function PersonNodeComponent({ data }: NodeProps<PersonFlowNode>) {
  // One person per selector: editing someone re-renders only their card.
  const person = useTreeStore((state) => state.tree.persons[data.personId]);
  if (!person) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} isConnectable={false} style={HIDDEN_HANDLE} />
      <PersonCard person={person} tape={data.tape} generation={data.generation} />
    </>
  );
}

export const PersonNode = memo(PersonNodeComponent);
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/UnionNode.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Handle, Position, type NodeProps } from '@xyflow/react';
import { memo, type CSSProperties } from 'react';
import { Stitch } from '../../shared/ui';
import type { UnionFlowNode } from './flowElements';

const HIDDEN_HANDLE: CSSProperties = { opacity: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' };

/** A marriage: the stitch between two cards, or a knot under a parent drawn alone. */
function UnionNodeComponent({ data, width = 0, height = 0 }: NodeProps<UnionFlowNode>) {
  return (
    <>
      <svg aria-hidden="true" width={width} height={height} className="overflow-visible">
        {data.couple ? (
          <Stitch left={{ x: 0, y: height / 2 }} right={{ x: width, y: height / 2 }} />
        ) : (
          <circle cx={width / 2} cy={height / 2} r={4} className="fill-thread" />
        )}
      </svg>
      <Handle type="source" position={Position.Bottom} isConnectable={false} style={HIDDEN_HANDLE} />
    </>
  );
}

export const UnionNode = memo(UnionNodeComponent);
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/ThreadEdge.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { EdgeProps } from '@xyflow/react';
import { memo } from 'react';
import { Thread } from '../../shared/ui';
import type { ThreadFlowEdge } from './flowElements';

/** Parent → child thread. The layout already knows the exact points, so handles are ignored. */
function ThreadEdgeComponent({ data }: EdgeProps<ThreadFlowEdge>) {
  if (!data) return null;
  return <Thread from={data.from} dropTo={data.dropTo} to={data.to} />;
}

export const ThreadEdge = memo(ThreadEdgeComponent);
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the board**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeBoard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ReactFlow, useReactFlow } from '@xyflow/react';
import '@xyflow/react/dist/base.css';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useReducedMotion } from '../../shared/ui';
import { toFlowElements, type FlowElements } from './flowElements';
import { layoutTree } from './layout';
import { PersonNode } from './PersonNode';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';
import { ThreadEdge } from './ThreadEdge';
import { UnionNode } from './UnionNode';

// Module constants: new objects on every render would make React Flow remount every node.
const nodeTypes = { person: PersonNode, union: UnionNode };
const edgeTypes = { thread: ThreadEdge };
const FIT_VIEW_OPTIONS = { maxZoom: 1, padding: 0.1 };

/** Brings new people into view after they are added. */
function FitWhenPeopleChange({ count }: { count: number }) {
  const { fitView } = useReactFlow();
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const first = useRef(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (first.current) {
      first.current = false;
      return;
    }
    const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      void fitView({ ...FIT_VIEW_OPTIONS, duration: reducedMotion ? 0 : 300 });
    });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [count, fitView, reducedMotion]);
  return null;
}

export function TreeBoard({ label }: { label: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const tree = useTreeStore((state) => state.tree);
  const layout = useMemo(() => layoutTree(tree), [tree]);
  const previous = useRef<FlowElements | undefined>(undefined);
  const elements = useMemo(() => toFlowElements(layout, previous.current), [layout]);
  useEffect(() => {
    previous.current = elements;
  }, [elements]);

  if (layout.persons.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-ink-muted">{t('tree.board.noProgenitor')}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label={label}
      className="board-grid h-[70vh] min-h-[420px] overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line"
    >
      <ReactFlow
        nodes={elements.nodes}
        edges={elements.edges}
        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
        edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
        nodesDraggable={false}
        nodesConnectable={false}
        elementsSelectable={false}
        fitView
        fitViewOptions={FIT_VIEW_OPTIONS}
        minZoom={0.2}
        maxZoom={2}
      >
        <FitWhenPeopleChange count={layout.persons.length} />
      </ReactFlow>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { act, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';

import { mockReactFlow } from '../../test/reactFlow';

import { resetTreeStore, useTreeStore } from './store';

const VIEWER = { ...CLAN, role: 'viewer' as const };

beforeEach(() => {
  mockReactFlow();
  resetTreeStore();
});

afterEach(() => {
  resetTreeStore();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('TreePage', () => {
  it('shows the board, the save status and the people not linked yet', async () => {
    mockApi(treeRoutes(FAMILY, VIEWER));
    renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`);
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('group', { name: 'Cây gia phả: Họ Nguyễn' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('Đã lưu');
    const tray = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Chưa nối vào cây' });
    expect(within(tray).getByText('Trần Văn Bình')).toBeInTheDocument();
    // Viewers get no editing controls.
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Thao tác với/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when the family tree is empty', async () => {
    mockApi(treeRoutes(EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE, VIEWER));
    renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Chưa có ai trong cây gia phả này.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows a load error with a way to try again', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let calls = 0;
    mockApi({
      ...treeRoutes(EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE, VIEWER),
      [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`]: () => {
        calls += 1;

    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockApi(treeRoutes(EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE, VIEWER));
    renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`);

    act(() => useTreeStore.setState({ notice: { kind: 'reloaded' } }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Đã tải lại bản mới nhất');
    act(() =>
      useTreeStore.setState({
        notice: {
          kind: 'rejected',
          error: new ApiError('RULE_VIOLATION', 'No', 422, undefined, 'CYCLE'),
        },
      }),
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(

    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đã hiểu' }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('alert')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

Replace `apps/web/src/features/tree/format.ts` with (Task 10's helpers plus `ruleKey` and `rejectionMessage`):
```ts
import type { DateInfo, OpErrorCode, Person } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import type { TFunction } from 'i18next';
import { ApiError, errorMessageKey } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';

/** The year on the side the user entered (approximate years are shown as plain years in P5). */
export function yearOf(date: DateInfo | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (!date) return undefined;
  const year =
    date.source === 'solar'
      ? date.solar?.year
      : date.source === 'lunar'
        ? date.lunar?.year
        : date.approxYear;
  return year === undefined ? undefined : String(year);
}

/** "1931–2004", "1962" for the living, "1931–?" or "?–2004" when one year is unknown. */
export function lifeYears(person: Person): string | undefined {
  const born = yearOf(person.birth);
  const died = person.death.isDeceased ? yearOf(person.death.date) : undefined;
  if (born !== undefined && died !== undefined) return `${born}–${died}`;
  if (born !== undefined) return person.death.isDeceased ? `${born}–?` : born;
  if (died !== undefined) return `?–${died}`;
  return undefined;
}

/** The card's accessible name: name, life years and generation (spec §7.8). */
export function cardLabel(
  t: TFunction,
  name: string,
  years: string | undefined,
  generation: number,
): string {
  return years === undefined
    ? t('tree.card.labelNoYears', { name, generation })
    : t('tree.card.label', { name, years, generation });
}

export function ruleKey(reason: OpErrorCode): `tree.rules.${OpErrorCode}` {
  return `tree.rules.${reason}`;
}

/** Why the server refused a change: the broken rule when it says which, else the error code. */
export function rejectionMessage(t: TFunction, error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.reason !== undefined) return t(ruleKey(error.reason));
  return t(errorMessageKey(error));
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/UnlinkedTray.tsx`:
```tsx
import { classifyLineage } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { Ellipsis } from 'lucide-react';
import { useContext, useId, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Button, Polaroid, tiltFor } from '../../shared/ui';
import { lifeYears } from './format';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';
import { TreeActionsContext } from './treeActions';

/** People not linked to the progenitor (spec §8 "unlinked"), with their actions for editors. */
export function UnlinkedTray() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const headingId = useId();
  const tree = useTreeStore((state) => state.tree);
  const openActions = useContext(TreeActionsContext);
  const people = useMemo(() => {
    const lineage = classifyLineage(tree);
    return Object.values(tree.persons)
      .filter((person) => lineage.get(person.id) === 'unlinked')
      .sort((a, b) => a.fullName.localeCompare(b.fullName, 'vi'));
  }, [tree]);

  if (people.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={headingId} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <h2 id={headingId} className="text-lg font-bold text-ink">
        {t('tree.unlinked.title')}
      </h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('tree.unlinked.hint')}</p>
      <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-4">
        {people.map((person) => {
          const years = lifeYears(person);
          return (
            <li key={person.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Polaroid
                lod="label"
                name={person.fullName}
                label={
                  years === undefined
                    ? person.fullName
                    : t('scrapbook.personLabel', { name: person.fullName, years })
                }
                lifeYears={years}
                tape="loose"
                tilt={tiltFor(person.id)}
              />
              {openActions ? (
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  size="icon"
                  className="size-8 rounded-full"
                  aria-label={t('tree.card.actions', { name: person.fullName })}
                  onClick={() => openActions(person.id)}
                >
                  <Ellipsis aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </Button>
              ) : null}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/SaveStatus.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';

/** Whether every change has reached the server (announced politely by screen readers). */
export function SaveStatus() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const pending = useTreeStore((state) => state.pending.length);
  const retrying = useTreeStore((state) => state.retrying);
  const text = retrying
    ? t('tree.save.retrying')
    : pending > 0
      ? t('tree.save.saving')
      : t('tree.save.saved');
  return (
    <p role="status" className="text-sm text-ink-muted">
      {text}
    </p>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeNoticeBanner.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui';
import { rejectionMessage } from './format';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';

/** After a 409 reload or a refused change, says what happened (spec §6.2–6.3). */
export function TreeNoticeBanner() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const notice = useTreeStore((state) => state.notice);
  const dismiss = useTreeStore((state) => state.dismissNotice);
  if (!notice) return null;
  const message =
    notice.kind === 'reloaded'
      ? t('tree.notice.reloaded')
      : t('tree.notice.rejected', { reason: rejectionMessage(t, notice.error) });
  return (
    <div
      role="alert"
      className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-control border border-stamp bg-surface p-3"
    >
      <p className="font-medium text-stamp">{message}</p>
      <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={dismiss}>
        {t('tree.notice.dismiss')}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Clan } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessageKey } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui';
import { EmptyTree } from './EmptyTree';
import { SaveStatus } from './SaveStatus';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';
import { TreeBoard } from './TreeBoard';
import { TreeNoticeBanner } from './TreeNoticeBanner';
import { UnlinkedTray } from './UnlinkedTray';

export function TreePage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const clan = useOutletContext<Clan>();
  const load = useTreeStore((state) => state.load);
  const loadedClanId = useTreeStore((state) => state.clanId);
  const status = useTreeStore((state) => state.status);
  const loadError = useTreeStore((state) => state.loadError);
  const isEmpty = useTreeStore((state) => Object.keys(state.tree.persons).length === 0);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load(clan.id);
  }, [clan.id, load]);

  if (loadedClanId === clan.id && status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <p role="alert" className="font-medium text-stamp">
          {t(errorMessageKey(loadError))}
        </p>
        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => void load(clan.id)}>
          {t('tree.retry')}
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (loadedClanId !== clan.id || status !== 'ready') {
    return <p className="text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <SaveStatus />
      <TreeNoticeBanner />
      {isEmpty ? (
        <EmptyTree />
      ) : (
        <>
          <TreeBoard label={t('tree.board.label', { name: clan.name })} />
          <UnlinkedTray />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 12: Editing — first person, child, spouse, delete, progenitor

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonFormDialog.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonActionsDialog.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/DeletePersonDialog.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeDialogs.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreePage.tsx`, `apps/web/src/locales/vi.json`, `apps/web/src/locales/en.json`
- Test: `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeEditing.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useTreeStore().apply(op): ApplyResult` (Task 8); `addFirstPersonOp`, `addChildOp`, `addSpouseOp`, `deletePersonOp`, `setProgenitorOp`, `NewPersonInput` (Task 8); `buildTreeIndex`, `FullNameSchema`, `GenderSchema`, `Person`, `OpErrorCode`; `ruleKey` (Task 11); `TreeActionsContext`, `EmptyTree({ onAddFirst })`; `useZodForm`; `Dialog`, `DialogClose`, `DialogContent`, `Button`, `TextField`; `hasRoleAtLeast`.
- Produces:
  - `type TreeDialog = { kind: 'first' } | { kind: 'actions' | 'child' | 'spouse' | 'delete'; personId: string } | null` and `TreeDialogs({ dialog, isOwner, onDialogChange })`.
  - `PersonFormDialog({ title, unionChoices?, onSubmit, onClose })` — full name, gender, living/deceased and, for a child of someone with several marriages, which marriage; `onSubmit(input, unionId) → ApplyResult`; a local rule error is shown in the dialog.
  - `PersonActionsDialog({ person, isOwner, isProgenitor, onAddChild, onAddSpouse, onSetProgenitor, onDelete, onClose })`.
  - `DeletePersonDialog({ person, onConfirm, onClose })` — shows the broken rule (for example `PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN`) instead of closing.
  - Final `TreePage`: editors and the Owner get the actions button on every card and in the tray, and "Add the first person" on an empty tree; only the Owner sees "Set as progenitor"; viewers get no editing controls.
  - Locale keys `tree.actions.*`, `tree.form.*`, `tree.delete.*`.

Every edit goes through `useTreeStore().apply`, so the card appears at once and `applyOp` refuses a broken rule before anything is sent (the API checks again).

```tsx
import type { TreeResponse } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { mockApi } from '../../test/api';
import { mockReactFlow } from '../../test/reactFlow';
import { renderRoute } from '../../test/render';
import {
  CLAN,
  CLAN_ID,
  EMPTY_TREE_RESPONSE,
  FAMILY,
  fixturePerson,
  GRANDPA,
  LOOSE,
  treeRoutes,
} from './fixtures';
import { resetTreeStore } from './store';

const OPS = `POST /clans/${CLAN_ID}/ops`;
const EDITOR = { ...CLAN, role: 'editor' as const };
const SECOND_WIFE = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a004';
const SECOND_UNION = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6b002';

/** Mocks the tree and accepts every op; returns the ops the page sent. */
function editableTree(tree: TreeResponse, clan = CLAN) {
  let seq = tree.version;
  const api = mockApi({
    ...treeRoutes(tree, clan),
    [OPS]: () => {
      seq += 1;
      return { body: { seq } };
    },
  });
  return () => api.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST').map((call) => call.body);
}

async function openActions(user: ReturnType<typeof userEvent.setup>, name: string) {

  return screen.getByRole('dialog', { name });
}

});

});

    await user.click(within(form).getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm vào cây' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('figure', { name: 'Đỗ Thị Lan, đời thứ 2' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'data-tape',
      'in-law',
    );
    await waitFor(() => expect(sent()).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(sent()[0]).toMatchObject({ op: { type: 'batch' } });
  });

  it('explains why someone cannot be deleted, and deletes someone who can be', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const sent = editableTree(FAMILY, EDITOR);
    renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`);

    const actions = await openActions(user, 'Lê Thị Hoa');
    await user.click(within(actions).getByRole('button', { name: 'Xoá người này' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Xoá Lê Thị Hoa?' });
    await user.click(within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: 'Xoá' }));
    expect(within(confirm).getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Không xoá được người còn con.',
    );
    await user.click(within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: 'Huỷ' }));
    expect(sent()).toEqual([]);

    const looseActions = await openActions(user, 'Trần Văn Bình');
    await user.click(within(looseActions).getByRole('button', { name: 'Xoá người này' }));
    await user.click(
      within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Xoá Trần Văn Bình?' })).getByRole('button', {
        name: 'Xoá',
      }),
    );
    await waitFor(() => expect(sent()).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(sent()[0]).toEqual({ baseVersion: 3, op: { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: LOOSE } } });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('region', { name: 'Chưa nối vào cây' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the dialogs**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonFormDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { FullNameSchema, GenderSchema } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { useId } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { useZodForm } from '../../shared/lib/formErrors';
import { Button, Dialog, DialogClose, DialogContent, TextField } from '../../shared/ui';
import { ruleKey } from './format';
import type { NewPersonInput } from './ops';
import type { ApplyResult } from './store';

const PersonFormSchema = z.object({
  fullName: FullNameSchema,
  gender: GenderSchema,
  status: z.enum(['living', 'deceased']),
  unionId: z.string().optional(),
});

const SELECT_CLASS =
  'h-10 rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-surface px-3 text-base text-ink';

export interface UnionChoice {
  id: string;
  label: string;
}

interface PersonFormDialogProps {
  title: string;
  /** Shown as a choice only when there are two or more. */
  unionChoices?: UnionChoice[];
  onSubmit: (input: NewPersonInput, unionId: string | undefined) => ApplyResult;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/** The P5 person form: full name, gender, living or deceased (dates arrive in P6). */
export function PersonFormDialog({
  title,
  unionChoices = [],
  onSubmit,
  onClose,
}: PersonFormDialogProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const unionFieldId = useId();
  const form = useZodForm(PersonFormSchema, {
    defaultValues: { fullName: '', gender: 'male', status: 'living', unionId: unionChoices[0]?.id },
  });
  const { errors } = form.formState;

  const submit = form.handleSubmit((values) => {
    const result = onSubmit(
      { fullName: values.fullName, gender: values.gender, isDeceased: values.status === 'deceased' },
      values.unionId,
    );
    if (result.ok) onClose();
    else form.setError('root.server', { type: 'rule', message: t(ruleKey(result.reason)) });
  });

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => (open ? undefined : onClose())}>
      <DialogContent title={title}>
        <form noValidate onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <TextField
            label={t('tree.form.fullName')}
            autoComplete="off"
            error={errors.fullName?.message}
            {...form.register('fullName')}
          />
          <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <legend className="mb-1 text-sm font-medium text-ink">{t('tree.form.gender')}</legend>
            <div className="flex gap-6">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input type="radio" value="male" className="size-4" {...form.register('gender')} />
                {t('tree.form.male')}
              </label>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input type="radio" value="female" className="size-4" {...form.register('gender')} />
                {t('tree.form.female')}
              </label>
            </div>
          </fieldset>
          <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <legend className="mb-1 text-sm font-medium text-ink">{t('tree.form.status')}</legend>
            <div className="flex gap-6">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input type="radio" value="living" className="size-4" {...form.register('status')} />
                {t('tree.form.living')}
              </label>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  value="deceased"
                  className="size-4"
                  {...form.register('status')}
                />
                {t('tree.form.deceased')}
              </label>
            </div>
          </fieldset>
          {unionChoices.length > 1 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <label htmlFor={unionFieldId} className="text-sm font-medium text-ink">
                {t('tree.form.union')}
              </label>
              <select id={unionFieldId} className={SELECT_CLASS} {...form.register('unionId')}>
                {unionChoices.map((choice) => (
                  <option key={choice.id} value={choice.id}>
                    {choice.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          ) : null}
          {errors.root?.server ? (
            <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-stamp">
              {errors.root.server.message}
            </p>
          ) : null}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <DialogClose asChild>
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost">
                {t('tree.form.cancel')}
              </Button>
            </DialogClose>
            <Button type="submit">{t('tree.form.submit')}</Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/PersonActionsDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Person } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { Baby, Crown, HeartPlus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Button, Dialog, DialogContent } from '../../shared/ui';

interface PersonActionsDialogProps {
  person: Person;
  isOwner: boolean;
  isProgenitor: boolean;
  onAddChild: () => void;
  onAddSpouse: () => void;
  onSetProgenitor: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const ICON = { 'aria-hidden': true, className: 'size-4', strokeWidth: 1.75 } as const;

export function PersonActionsDialog({
  person,
  isOwner,
  isProgenitor,
  onAddChild,
  onAddSpouse,
  onSetProgenitor,
  onDelete,
  onClose,
}: PersonActionsDialogProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => (open ? undefined : onClose())}>
      <DialogContent title={person.fullName} description={t('tree.actions.description')}>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" className="justify-start" onClick={onAddChild}>
            <Baby {...ICON} />
            {t('tree.actions.addChild')}
          </Button>
          <Button variant="secondary" className="justify-start" onClick={onAddSpouse}>
            <HeartPlus {...ICON} />
            {t('tree.actions.addSpouse')}
          </Button>
          {isOwner && !isProgenitor ? (
            <Button variant="secondary" className="justify-start" onClick={onSetProgenitor}>
              <Crown {...ICON} />
              {t('tree.actions.setProgenitor')}
            </Button>
          ) : null}
          <Button variant="danger" className="justify-start" onClick={onDelete}>
            <Trash2 {...ICON} />
            {t('tree.actions.delete')}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/DeletePersonDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { OpErrorCode, Person } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Button, Dialog, DialogClose, DialogContent } from '../../shared/ui';
import { ruleKey } from './format';
import type { ApplyResult } from './store';

interface DeletePersonDialogProps {
  person: Person;
  onConfirm: () => ApplyResult;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/** Confirms a delete; when a rule forbids it (spec §6.1), says which and stays open. */
export function DeletePersonDialog({ person, onConfirm, onClose }: DeletePersonDialogProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [reason, setReason] = useState<OpErrorCode | null>(null);
  const confirm = () => {
    const result = onConfirm();
    if (result.ok) onClose();
    else setReason(result.reason);
  };
  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => (open ? undefined : onClose())}>
      <DialogContent
        title={t('tree.delete.title', { name: person.fullName })}
        description={t('tree.delete.body', { name: person.fullName })}
      >
        {reason ? (
          <p role="alert" className="mb-4 font-medium text-stamp">
            {t(ruleKey(reason))}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <DialogClose asChild>
            <Button variant="ghost">{t('tree.delete.cancel')}</Button>
          </DialogClose>
          <Button variant="danger" onClick={confirm}>
            {t('tree.delete.confirm')}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeDialogs.tsx`:
```tsx
import { buildTreeIndex } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { DeletePersonDialog } from './DeletePersonDialog';
import {
  addChildOp,
  addFirstPersonOp,
  addSpouseOp,
  deletePersonOp,
  setProgenitorOp,
} from './ops';
import { PersonActionsDialog } from './PersonActionsDialog';
import { PersonFormDialog, type UnionChoice } from './PersonFormDialog';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';

export type TreeDialog =
  | { kind: 'first' }
  | { kind: 'actions' | 'child' | 'spouse' | 'delete'; personId: string }
  | null;

interface TreeDialogsProps {
  dialog: TreeDialog;
  isOwner: boolean;
  onDialogChange: (next: TreeDialog) => void;
}

/** One dialog at a time; each is mounted only while open, so its form starts empty. */
export function TreeDialogs({ dialog, isOwner, onDialogChange }: TreeDialogsProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const tree = useTreeStore((state) => state.tree);
  const apply = useTreeStore((state) => state.apply);
  const index = useMemo(() => buildTreeIndex(tree), [tree]);
  const close = () => onDialogChange(null);

  if (dialog === null) return null;
  if (dialog.kind === 'first') {
    return (
      <PersonFormDialog
        title={t('tree.form.firstTitle')}
        onSubmit={(input) => apply(addFirstPersonOp(input))}
        onClose={close}
      />
    );
  }
  const person = tree.persons[dialog.personId];
  // Deleted meanwhile (for example by a reload after a conflict).
  if (!person) return null;

  switch (dialog.kind) {
    case 'actions':
      return (
        <PersonActionsDialog
          person={person}
          isOwner={isOwner}
          isProgenitor={tree.progenitorId === person.id}
          onAddChild={() => onDialogChange({ kind: 'child', personId: person.id })}
          onAddSpouse={() => onDialogChange({ kind: 'spouse', personId: person.id })}
          onSetProgenitor={() => {
            if (apply(setProgenitorOp(person.id)).ok) close();
          }}
          onDelete={() => onDialogChange({ kind: 'delete', personId: person.id })}
          onClose={close}
        />
      );
    case 'child': {
      const choices: UnionChoice[] = index.unionsOf(person.id).map((union) => {
        const partnerId = union.partnerIds.find((id) => id !== person.id);
        const partner = partnerId === undefined ? undefined : tree.persons[partnerId];
        return {
          id: union.id,
          label: partner
            ? t('tree.form.unionWith', { name: person.fullName, partner: partner.fullName })
            : t('tree.form.unionAlone', { name: person.fullName }),
        };
      });
      return (
        <PersonFormDialog
          title={t('tree.form.childTitle', { name: person.fullName })}
          unionChoices={choices}
          onSubmit={(input, unionId) => apply(addChildOp(index, person.id, input, unionId))}
          onClose={close}
        />
      );
    }
    case 'spouse':
      return (
        <PersonFormDialog
          title={t('tree.form.spouseTitle', { name: person.fullName })}
          onSubmit={(input) => apply(addSpouseOp(index, person.id, input))}
          onClose={close}
        />
      );
    case 'delete':
      return (
        <DeletePersonDialog
          person={person}
          onConfirm={() => apply(deletePersonOp(person.id))}
          onClose={close}
        />
      );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Turn on editing in the page**

Replace `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreePage.tsx` with:
```tsx
import { hasRoleAtLeast, type Clan } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessageKey } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui';
import { EmptyTree } from './EmptyTree';
import { SaveStatus } from './SaveStatus';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';
import { TreeActionsContext } from './treeActions';
import { TreeBoard } from './TreeBoard';
import { TreeDialogs, type TreeDialog } from './TreeDialogs';
import { TreeNoticeBanner } from './TreeNoticeBanner';
import { UnlinkedTray } from './UnlinkedTray';

export function TreePage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const clan = useOutletContext<Clan>();
  const load = useTreeStore((state) => state.load);
  const loadedClanId = useTreeStore((state) => state.clanId);
  const status = useTreeStore((state) => state.status);
  const loadError = useTreeStore((state) => state.loadError);
  const isEmpty = useTreeStore((state) => Object.keys(state.tree.persons).length === 0);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<TreeDialog>(null);
  // Stable, so the cards (context consumers) do not re-render when the page does.
  const openActions = useCallback(
    (personId: string) => setDialog({ kind: 'actions', personId }),
    [],
  );
  const canEdit = hasRoleAtLeast(clan.role, 'editor');

  useEffect(() => {
    void load(clan.id);
  }, [clan.id, load]);

  if (loadedClanId === clan.id && status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <p role="alert" className="font-medium text-stamp">
          {t(errorMessageKey(loadError))}
        </p>
        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => void load(clan.id)}>
          {t('tree.retry')}
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (loadedClanId !== clan.id || status !== 'ready') {
    return <p className="text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <TreeActionsContext value={canEdit ? openActions : null}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <SaveStatus />
        <TreeNoticeBanner />
        {isEmpty ? (
          <EmptyTree onAddFirst={canEdit ? () => setDialog({ kind: 'first' }) : undefined} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <TreeBoard label={t('tree.board.label', { name: clan.name })} />
            <UnlinkedTray />
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      {canEdit ? (
        <TreeDialogs dialog={dialog} isOwner={clan.role === 'owner'} onDialogChange={setDialog} />
      ) : null}
    </TreeActionsContext>
  );
}
```

In `apps/web/src/features/clans/api.ts`, after `useUpdateClan`, add:
```ts
export function useDeleteClan(id: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => apiRequest(`/clans/${id}`, z.undefined(), { method: 'DELETE' }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      // The family tree is gone: drop its cached queries instead of refetching a 404.
      queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: clanKeys.detail(id) });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: clanKeys.all, exact: true });
    },
  });
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/clans/DeleteClanSection.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Clan } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { useId, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessageKey } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { Button, Dialog, DialogClose, DialogContent, TextField } from '../../shared/ui';
import { useDeleteClan } from './api';

/** The Owner deletes the whole family tree (spec §3); typing its name guards against a slip. */
export function DeleteClanSection({ clan }: { clan: Clan }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const deleteClan = useDeleteClan(clan.id);
  const headingId = useId();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState('');
  const matches = typed.trim() === clan.name;

  const onOpenChange = (next: boolean) => {
    setOpen(next);
    if (!next) {
      setTyped('');
      deleteClan.reset();
    }
  };

  const confirm = async () => {
    try {
      await deleteClan.mutateAsync();
    } catch {
      return; // Shown in the dialog through deleteClan.error.
    }
    void navigate('/clans', { replace: true });
  };

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby={headingId}
      className="flex max-w-xl flex-col items-start gap-3 rounded-photo border border-stamp/40 p-4"
    >
      <h2 id={headingId} className="text-lg font-bold text-ink">
        {t('clans.delete.title')}
      </h2>
      <p className="text-ink-muted">{t('clans.delete.body')}</p>
      <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => onOpenChange(true)}>
        {t('clans.delete.open')}
      </Button>
      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
        <DialogContent
          title={t('clans.delete.confirmTitle', { name: clan.name })}
          description={t('clans.delete.body')}
        >
          <form
            noValidate
            className="flex flex-col gap-4"
            onSubmit={(event) => {
              event.preventDefault();
              if (matches) void confirm();
            }}
          >
            <TextField
              label={t('clans.delete.confirmLabel', { name: clan.name })}
              autoComplete="off"
              value={typed}
              onChange={(event) => setTyped(event.target.value)}
            />
            {deleteClan.error ? (
              <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-stamp">
                {t(errorMessageKey(deleteClan.error))}
              </p>
            ) : null}
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <DialogClose asChild>
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost">
                  {t('clans.delete.cancel')}
                </Button>
              </DialogClose>
              <Button type="submit" variant="danger" disabled={!matches || deleteClan.isPending}>
                {t('clans.delete.confirm')}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </section>
  );
}
```

Create `e2e/support.ts`:
```ts
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';

export const PASSWORD = 'correct horse 1';

export function uniqueEmail(name: string): string {
  return `${name}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}@example.test`;
}

/** Fills the sign-up form on the current page; returns the new account's email. */
export async function register(page: Page, name: string, slug: string): Promise<string> {
  const email = uniqueEmail(slug);
  await page.getByLabel('Tên hiển thị').fill(name);
  await page.getByLabel('Email').fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel('Mật khẩu', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tạo tài khoản' }).click();
  return email;
}
```

```ts
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { register } from './support';

/** Fills the person form that is open and adds the person. */
async function addPerson(page: Page, fullName: string, gender: 'Nam' | 'Nữ' = 'Nam') {
  const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Họ và tên').fill(fullName);
  await dialog.getByRole('radio', { name: gender }).check();
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm vào cây' }).click();
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden();
}

async function openActions(page: Page, name: string) {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: `Thao tác với ${name}` }).click();
  return page.getByRole('dialog', { name });
}

test('build a small family tree and find it again after a reload', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/register');
  await register(page, 'Minh', 'minh');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Cây gia phả của tôi' })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByLabel('Tên cây gia phả').fill('Họ Phạm');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tạo cây gia phả' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/clans\/[0-9a-f]{24}\/tree$/);
  await expect(page.getByText('Chưa có ai trong cây gia phả này.')).toBeVisible();

  // The first person waits in the tray until the Manager makes them the progenitor.
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm người đầu tiên' }).click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả');
  const tray = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Chưa nối vào cây' });
  await expect(tray.getByText('Phạm Văn Cả')).toBeVisible();
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả'))
    .getByRole('button', { name: 'Chọn làm thuỷ tổ' })
    .click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Cả, đời thứ 1' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(tray).toBeHidden();

  // A child first (a one-parent union), then a spouse who joins that union.
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm con' }).click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Phạm Văn Hai');
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả'))
    .getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm vợ/chồng' })
    .click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Nguyễn Thị Ba', 'Nữ');
  await expect(page.getByText('Đã lưu', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Cả, đời thứ 1' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Thị Ba, đời thứ 1' })).toHaveAttribute(
    'data-tape',
    'in-law',
  );
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Hai, đời thứ 2' })).toBeVisible();
  // React Flow draws the thread once the cards are measured; the couple shares one stitch.
  await expect(page.locator('[data-relation="parent-child"]')).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(page.locator('[data-relation="marriage"]')).toHaveCount(1);

  const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page })
    .withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21a', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa'])
    .analyze();
  const serious = results.violations
    .filter((violation) => violation.impact === 'serious' || violation.impact === 'critical')
    .map((violation) => `${violation.id}: ${violation.help} (${violation.nodes.length})`);
  expect(serious).toEqual([]);
});

test('the Manager deletes the family tree', async ({ page }) => {

  await page.getByLabel('Tên cây gia phả').fill('Họ Đỗ');

  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xoá vĩnh viễn' }).click();

  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Cây gia phả của tôi' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: /Họ Đỗ/ })).toHaveCount(0);
});
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: documentation only. No code changes, so there is no failing test in this task; `pnpm check` (Prettier also formats Markdown) is the gate.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write ADR 0007**

Create `docs/adr/0007-operation-log-and-optimistic-edits.md`:
```markdown
# ADR 0007: One `applyOp` for browser and server, an operation log and optimistic edits

- Status: Accepted
- Date: 2026-11-13

## Context

A family tree is edited by several relatives, often on weak mobile connections. An edit must show at once, the server must refuse anything that breaks a rule (a cycle, deleting someone who still has children), two people saving at the same time must not overwrite each other, and P6 needs undo, a change history and "revert to this point". Rules written twice, once in the browser and once in the API, would drift apart.

## Decision

**One pure function.** `applyOp(tree, op) → { tree, inverse }` in `packages/shared/src/tree/` (imported as `@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`) holds every business rule. It is immutable with structural sharing: it copies only the records it changes. It throws `OpError` with a code (`PERSON_HAS_CHILDREN`, `CYCLE`, …) when a rule is broken. The browser runs it before sending, so a broken rule is explained at once without a round trip; the API runs the same function again and answers `RULE_VIOLATION` (422) with the code as `reason`.

**Every change is an operation with an inverse.** `addPerson`, `updatePerson`, `deletePerson`, `addUnion`, `updateUnion`, `deleteUnion`, `setParents`, `setProgenitor` and `batch` (1–500 ops, not nested); `restorePerson`/`restoreUnion` exist only as inverses. The inverse is computed from the state before the change, so applying it restores the tree exactly. A `batch` inverse is the inverses in reverse order.

**An append-only log and a version per family tree.** `POST /api/clans/:id/ops { op, baseVersion }` runs in one MongoDB transaction: compare `Clan.version` with `baseVersion` (409 `VERSION_CONFLICT` if different), load the tree, run `applyOp`, write only the Person and Union documents whose object changed (found by reference, thanks to structural sharing), insert the Operation `{ seq: version + 1, op, inverse }` and set `Clan.version = seq`. The unique `(clanId, seq)` index turns any race the version check misses into a duplicate key, also answered with 409. `GET /tree` reads in a snapshot transaction, so the version always matches the data.

**Optimistic edits in the browser.** The Zustand store keeps the confirmed tree and version, a queue of pending ops and the optimistic tree (confirmed tree plus pending ops). Ops are sent one at a time, each with the version the previous one produced. A network failure keeps the queue and retries with backoff (1 s, 2 s, 4 s, … up to 30 s). A 409 reloads the whole tree and tells the user which edits were not saved; a 4xx drops the refused op, tells the user why and replays the rest on the confirmed tree.

## Consequences

- The browser and the API cannot disagree about a rule, and the rules are tested once, with property tests that check every inverse.
- The log gives P6 undo (send the inverse), history and "revert to this point" without another data model.
- Ids are generated in the browser (ObjectIds), so a new card has its final id before the server answers; the API checks the id format with Zod and that the id is unused (`DUPLICATE_ID`).
- In P5, a 409 throws away unsaved edits after telling the user. P6 replaces the reload with the rebase of spec §6.3 (fetch the missed ops, replay the pending ones on top).
- A write loads the whole tree (up to the 5,000-person design limit). That is fast enough for P5 and P8; P7 measures it.
- Every write is a transaction, so MongoDB must run as a replica set: Atlas does, and tests use `MongoMemoryReplSet`.
```

```
docs: record the operation log, P5 limits and follow-ups

ADR 0007 explains the shared applyOp, the operation log with a
version per family tree and the optimistic store. The spec (EN and
VI) now says that malformed ids are validation errors, that a union
with children cannot be deleted, that rule violations carry a reason
and what the batch and tree limits are; /clans/:id opens the tree.
```

## Self-Review

**Spec coverage** (roadmap P5 row and carry-over; spec sections in the header)
- §5 Person, Union, Operation, `Clan.version`/`progenitorId`: Tasks 1, 2, 5. Fields that belong to later sub-plans (`treeLink`, `photo`, `cardStyle`, `TreeTheme`) are left out on purpose and listed in the user decisions.
- §6.1 operations, inverses and business rules (including `setProgenitor`, the progenitor protection, no cycles, no delete of a person or union with children): Task 2, with property tests that every inverse restores the tree. Rule reasons in the error contract: Task 4.
- §6.2 write flow (transaction, version check, 409, operation log, `GET /ops?since=`): Tasks 5 and 6. §6.3 is reduced to "reload on 409" as agreed; the rebase is a P6 roadmap item (Task 15).
- §3 permissions: editors write, the Owner alone sets the progenitor (API in Task 6, UI in Task 12) and deletes a family tree (API in Task 7, UI in Task 13); viewers see no editing controls (Tasks 11 and 12).
- §7.2 routes: `/clans/:id/tree`, `/clans/:id` → tree, the Tree tab, signed-in `/` → `/clans` (Task 11).
- §7.3 store: optimistic tree, pending queue, retry with backoff, "Unsaved" status (Tasks 8 and 11).
- §7.4 steps 1–4 (layout on the main thread, memoised nodes, stable selectors); the Web Worker, LOD switching and measurements stay in P7.
- §8 classification: generations, blood/in-law/unlinked, tape colours by branch, the "Not yet linked" tray (Tasks 3, 9, 10, 11).
- §13.1–13.3: rule reasons translated, empty, loading and error states, scrapbook primitives on the board (Tasks 4, 10, 11).
- §15: unit, property, API (with `MongoMemoryReplSet`), component and Playwright tests (Task 14), axe on the tree page.
- Carry-over "Delete a clan (Owner) together with its people and operations": Tasks 7, 13, ticked in Task 15.

**Placeholder scan.** No "TBD", "TODO", "similar to Task N" or unspecified error handling. Every code step has the full code; the two places that edit an existing file in place (Task 13 `ClanSettingsPage.tsx`, Task 14 `accounts.spec.ts`) quote the exact text to replace. Task 15 has no test because it changes only Markdown; `pnpm check` (Prettier) is its gate.

**Type and name consistency** (checked across tasks)
- `@ancestors-tree/shared/tree` exports used later: `newObjectId`, `isObjectId`, `PersonSchema`, `FullNameSchema`, `GenderSchema`, `Person`, `Union`, `TreeState`, `treeStateFromLists`, `CLEARABLE_PERSON_FIELDS`, `Op`, `SingleOp`, `OpError`, `OpErrorCode`, `OP_ERROR_CODE_LIST`, `applyOp`, `needsOwner`, `MAX_BATCH_OPS`, `buildTreeIndex`/`TreeIndex` (`unionsOf`, `childrenOf`), `computeGenerations`, `classifyLineage`.
- `ApiError(code, message, status, details?, reason?)` (Task 4) is what Task 11's notice test builds; `AppError(code, message, details?, reason?)` is what Task 6 throws.
- Store API (Task 8) used by Tasks 10–12: `useTreeStore` with `clanId`, `status`, `loadError`, `tree`, `pending`, `retrying`, `notice`, `load`, `apply(op) → ApplyResult`, `dismissNotice`; `resetTreeStore()` in every test that touches the store.
- Op builders (Task 8) used by Task 12: `addFirstPersonOp(input)`, `addChildOp(index, parentId, input, unionId?)`, `addSpouseOp(index, personId, input)`, `deletePersonOp(id)`, `setProgenitorOp(id)`, `NewPersonInput { fullName, gender, isDeceased }`.
- `TreeActionsContext` (Task 10) is provided by the final `TreePage` (Task 12) and read by `PersonCard` and `UnlinkedTray` (Task 11).
- `ruleKey` and `rejectionMessage` arrive with Task 11's `format.ts` and are used by Tasks 11 and 12.
- Locale keys: `tree.rules.*` (Task 4), `tree.board.*` and `tree.card.*` (Task 10), `tree.empty`, `tree.retry`, `tree.unlinked.*`, `tree.save.*`, `tree.notice.*`, `clans.tabs.tree` and `tree.actions.addFirst` (Task 11, which `EmptyTree` needs for the typecheck), the rest of `tree.actions.*` with `tree.form.*` and `tree.delete.*` (Task 12, which replaces the `tree.actions` object), `clans.delete.*` (Task 13). Every key is added to `vi.json` and `en.json` in the same step.
- `CLAN_SCOPED_MODEL_NAMES` and `deleteClanData` (Task 7) match the File Structure; `scoped.test.ts` fails if a new model with a `clanId` path is not listed.
- Test fixtures: `features/tree/fixtures.ts` (Task 8) ids are unique 24-hex strings; Task 12 adds `64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a004` and `…b002`, which no other fixture uses. E-mail addresses use `example.test`; the e2e password is the existing test value from `accounts.spec.ts`.

**Known limits written into the plan** (reported to the user, not bugs)
- Layout: a marriage between two blood relatives is a knot under the partner reached first; an in-law married to two blood relatives is drawn once; with three or more marriages, stitches pass behind the cards in between. All three are P7 roadmap items (Task 15).
- Component tests do not assert edges (React Flow draws them only after the browser measures nodes); the Playwright test checks the thread and the stitch.
- Leaving the tree page with unsent edits keeps them in the store, which keeps sending them; reopening the same family tree shows them.
